function box(parent, width, height, depth, mat, x, y, z, shadow = false) {
  const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(width, height, depth), mat);
  mesh.position.set(x, y, z);
  mesh.castShadow = shadow;
  mesh.receiveShadow = shadow;
  parent.add(mesh);
  return mesh;
}

function cylinder(parent, top, bottom, height, mat, x, y, z, segments = 10) {
  const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(top, bottom, height, segments), mat);
  mesh.position.set(x, y, z);
  parent.add(mesh);
  return mesh;
}

function sphere(parent, radius, mat, x, y, z, sx = 1, sy = 1, sz = 1) {
  const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(radius, 14, 10), mat);
  mesh.position.set(x, y, z);
  mesh.scale.set(sx, sy, sz);
  parent.add(mesh);
  return mesh;
}

function makeFlag(width = 0.75, height = width * (267 / 400)) {
  const flag = new THREE.Group();
  const cloth = new THREE.Mesh(
    new THREE.BoxGeometry(width, height, 0.02),
    flagMaterial
  );
  flag.add(cloth);
  return flag;
}


function decorateFestivalNpc(mesh, npc) {
  const data = mesh.userData;
  const head = data.face.parent;
  if (npc.gtLook === 'woman') {
	for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) box(data.torso, 0.455, 0.045, 0.235, material(PALETTE[(npc.id + i * 2) % PALETTE.length]), 0, 0.14 - i * 0.12, 0);
	const skirt = box(data.hip, 0.5, 0.5, 0.32, material(npc.gtSkirt), 0, -0.2, 0);
	box(skirt, 0.51, 0.06, 0.33, material(PALETTE[(npc.id + 3) % PALETTE.length]), 0, -0.1, 0);
	box(skirt, 0.51, 0.04, 0.33, material(PALETTE[(npc.id + 5) % PALETTE.length]), 0, 0.12, 0);
	box(head, 0.07, 0.36, 0.07, material(0x0b0b0d), 0, -0.16, -0.14);
  } else if (npc.gtHat === 'straw') {
	cylinder(head, 0.27, 0.27, 0.025, material(0xd9b66b), 0, 0.14, 0, 14);
	cylinder(head, 0.14, 0.15, 0.12, material(0xc9a24f), 0, 0.2, 0, 12);
	cylinder(head, 0.151, 0.151, 0.03, material(BLUE), 0, 0.165, 0, 12);
  } else if (npc.gtHat === 'cap') {
	box(head, 0.27, 0.07, 0.27, material(BLUE), 0, 0.14, 0);
	box(head, 0.2, 0.03, 0.14, material(BLUE), 0, 0.12, 0.2);
  }
  if (npc.gtSash) {
	const sash = box(data.torso, 0.58, 0.07, 0.24, material(npc.gtSash), 0, 0.02, 0);
	sash.rotation.z = 0.85;
  }
  if (npc.gtRole === 'flag') {
	box(data.hip, 0.035, 1.5, 0.035, material(0x7c5a35), 0.31, 0.42, 0.16);
	const flag = makeFlag();
	flag.position.set(0.685, 1.0, 0.16);
	data.hip.add(flag);
	data.flag = flag;
  }
}

function addFestivalNpc(def, category) {
  const id = npcs.length;
  const npc = {
	id, name: def.name, role: 'stationary', aggro: 'passive', clanId: null,
	homeX: def.x, homeZ: def.z, x: def.x, z: def.z, yaw: def.yaw || 0,
	shirt: def.shirt, pants: def.pants, bandana: null, line: `"${def.line}"`,
	age: 'adult', skin: def.skin, hair: def.hair, wallet: 0, picked: false, pickCool: 0,
	fleeTimer: 0, kvx: 0, kvz: 0, mesh: null, spawned: false, walkCycle: Math.random() * 6,
	turnTimer: 3, stunTimer: 0, hostile: false, alertState: 'patrol', searchX: def.x,
	searchZ: def.z, searchTimer: 0, attackCooldown: 0, punchTimer: 0, punchSide: 0,
	hp: 100, maxHp: 100, asleep: false, sleepTimer: 0, sleepZGroup: null,
	scale: def.scale || 1, gtFestival: true, gtCategory: category, gtLook: def.look || 'man', gtHat: def.hat,
	gtSkirt: def.skirt, gtSash: def.sash, gtRole: def.gtRole, gtHome: { x: def.x, z: def.z }, gtYaw: def.yaw || 0
  };
  npcs.push(npc);
  if (category === 'parade') festival.parade.push(npc);
  else festival.homers.push(npc);
  return npc;
}

const PR = 8;
const STRAIGHT = 32;
const ARC = Math.PI / 2 * PR;
const LOOP = 4 * STRAIGHT + 4 * ARC;
const SEGMENTS = [
  { line: true, x: -16, z: -24, dx: 1, dz: 0 }, { cx: 16, cz: -16, a0: -Math.PI / 2 },
  { line: true, x: 24, z: -16, dx: 0, dz: 1 }, { cx: 16, cz: 16, a0: 0 },
  { line: true, x: 16, z: 24, dx: -1, dz: 0 }, { cx: -16, cz: 16, a0: Math.PI / 2 },
  { line: true, x: -24, z: 16, dx: 0, dz: -1 }, { cx: -16, cz: -16, a0: Math.PI }
];

function pathAt(distance) {
  let remaining = ((distance % LOOP) + LOOP) % LOOP;
  for (const segment of SEGMENTS) {
	const length = segment.line ? STRAIGHT : ARC;
	if (remaining <= length) {
	  if (segment.line) return { x: segment.x + segment.dx * remaining, z: segment.z + segment.dz * remaining, hx: segment.dx, hz: segment.dz };
	  const angle = segment.a0 + remaining / ARC * Math.PI / 2;
	  return { x: segment.cx + PR * Math.cos(angle), z: segment.cz + PR * Math.sin(angle), hx: -Math.sin(angle), hz: Math.cos(angle) };
	}
	remaining -= length;
  }
  return { x: -16, z: -24, hx: 1, hz: 0 };
}

function paradeSlot(npc) {
  const point = pathAt(festival.lead - 9 - npc.gtRow * 2.7 + npc.gtJitter);
  const lateral = (npc.gtColumn - 2) * 1.55;
  return { x: point.x - point.hz * lateral, z: point.z + point.hx * lateral, yaw: Math.atan2(point.hx, point.hz) };
}

function addFloatBase(group, bodyColor) {
  box(group, 3, 0.5, 6, material(bodyColor), 0, 0.95, 0, true);
  box(group, 3.1, 0.12, 6.1, material(WHITE), 0, 1.22, 0);
  for (const sx of [-1, 1]) for (const sz of [-1, 1]) {
	const wheel = cylinder(group, 0.45, 0.45, 0.3, material(0x111827), sx * 1.5, 0.45, sz * 2, 14);
	wheel.rotation.z = Math.PI / 2;
  }
  for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) {
	const z = -2.8 + i * 0.7;
	const color = PALETTE[i % PALETTE.length];
	sphere(group, 0.17, material(color), -1.55, 1.3, z);
	sphere(group, 0.17, material(color), 1.55, 1.3, z);
  }
  for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
	const sign = new THREE.Group();
	sign.rotation.y = side * Math.PI / 2;
	sign.position.set(side * 1.53, 0.92, 0);
	createNeonSign(sign, 'FIESTA GUATEMALTECA', 0, 0, 0, '#1d6fb8', '#ffffff', 3.4, 0.7);
	group.add(sign);
  }
}

function buildQuetzalFloat() {
  const group = new THREE.Group();
  addFloatBase(group, BLUE);
  box(group, 2.2, 0.5, 0.9, material(0x8b5a2b), 0, 1.55, 2.1, true);
  for (let i = 0; i < 12; i++) box(group, 0.15, 0.05, 0.7, material(i % 2 ? 0xc47f3a : 0xe0a35a), -0.98 + i * 0.178, 1.83, 2.1);
  cylinder(group, 0.12, 0.12, 2.2, material(0x6b4a2f), 0, 2.6, -0.4, 8).rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
  cylinder(group, 0.1, 0.14, 1.3, material(0x6b4a2f), 0, 1.9, -0.4, 8);
  for (const [x, color] of [[-0.55, BLUE], [0.55, WHITE]]) {
	const musician = buildNpcMeshRaw(color, 0x1e293b, null, { id: 700 + festival.musicians.length, age: 'adult', scale: 0.85, skin: SKIN[0], hair: HAIR[0] });
	musician.position.set(x, 1.28, 1.35);
	group.add(musician);
	festival.musicians.push(musician);
  }
  sphere(group, 0.75, material(0x16a34a), 0, 3.15, -0.35, 1, 1, 1.35);
  sphere(group, 0.58, material(0xdc2626), 0, 2.95, 0.3, 1, 0.9, 0.9);
  sphere(group, 0.4, material(0x16a34a), 0, 3.8, 0.55);
  sphere(group, 0.22, material(0x16a34a), 0, 4.2, 0.4, 0.8, 1, 1.4);
  box(group, 0.1, 0.1, 0.28, material(0xfacc15), 0, 3.78, 0.95);
  sphere(group, 0.06, material(0x0b0b0d), 0.2, 3.9, 0.82);
  sphere(group, 0.06, material(0x0b0b0d), -0.2, 3.9, 0.82);
  for (let row = 0; row < 3; row++) for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
	const z = -1.2 - i * 0.55;
	const y = Math.max(1.35, 2.9 - Math.sin(i * 0.45) * 0.55 - i * 0.18);
	box(group, 0.2 - i * 0.012, 0.07, 0.62, material(row === 1 ? 0x0d9488 : 0x16a34a), (row - 1) * 0.28 + Math.sin(i * 0.7 + row) * 0.18, y, z);
  }
  box(group, 1.9, 0.6, 0.12, material(0x16a34a), 0, 1.15, -3.05);
  group.position.y = 0.18;
  worldRoot.add(group);
  return group;
}

function buildTikalFloat() {
  const group = new THREE.Group();
  addFloatBase(group, 0x16a34a);
  const stone = material(0x9ca3a0), stone2 = material(0x7f8a86);
  [[2.6, 0.55], [2.1, 0.55], [1.6, 0.55], [1.1, 0.5]].forEach(([size, height], i) => box(group, size, height, size, i % 2 ? stone2 : stone, 0, 1.5 + i * 0.55 + height / 2 - 0.1, 0, true));
  box(group, 0.8, 0.8, 0.8, stone, 0, 3.95, 0, true);
  box(group, 0.3, 0.45, 0.04, material(0x111827), 0, 3.85, 0.42);
  box(group, 1, 0.18, 1, stone2, 0, 4.45, 0);
  for (const [x, z] of [[-1.3, -2.6], [1.3, -2.6], [-1.3, 2.6], [1.3, 2.6]]) {
	box(group, 0.04, 1.4, 0.04, material(0x7c5a35), x, 1.9, z);
	const flag = makeFlag(0.6, 0.38);
	flag.position.set(x + 0.3, 2.35, z);
	group.add(flag);
  }
  box(group, 0.06, 3.6, 0.06, material(0x7c5a35), 0, 3, -2.6);
  const bigFlag = makeFlag(2, 1.25);
  bigFlag.position.set(1, 4.2, -2.6);
  group.add(bigFlag);
  group.position.y = 0.18;
  worldRoot.add(group);
  return group;
}

function buildBunting() {
  const points = [[-30, -30], [30, -30], [30, 30], [-30, 30], [-18, -18], [18, -18], [18, 18], [-18, 18]];
  points.forEach(([x, z]) => cylinder(worldRoot, 0.07, 0.09, 5.4, material(0x475569), x, 2.9, z, 6));
  const lines = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
	lines.push([points[i], points[(i + 1) % 4]], [points[4 + i], points[4 + (i + 1) % 4]]);
  }
  const colors = [BLUE, WHITE, 0xfacc15, 0xdc2626, 0x16a34a, 0xf97316];
  for (const [[ax, az], [bx, bz]] of lines) {
	const dx = bx - ax, dz = bz - az, length = Math.hypot(dx, dz), angle = Math.atan2(dz, dx);
	const ropePoints = [];
	for (let i = 0; i <= 24; i++) {
	  const t = i / 24;
	  ropePoints.push(new THREE.Vector3(ax + dx * t, 5.1 - 0.55 * Math.sin(Math.PI * t), az + dz * t));
	}
	worldRoot.add(new THREE.Line(new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(ropePoints), new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: 0x111827 })));
	const geometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
	geometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute([-0.26, 0, 0, 0.26, 0, 0, 0, -0.52, 0], 3));
	geometry.computeVertexNormals();
	const count = Math.floor(length / 1.5);
	for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
	  const t = (i + 0.5) / count;
	  const pennant = new THREE.Mesh(geometry, new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: colors[i % colors.length], side: THREE.DoubleSide }));
	  pennant.position.set(ax + dx * t, 5.1 - 0.55 * Math.sin(Math.PI * t), az + dz * t);
	  pennant.rotation.y = -angle;
	  worldRoot.add(pennant);
	}
  }
}

function buildCampero() {
  const x0 = 49, x1 = 60, z0 = -10, z1 = -2, height = 4, thickness = 0.3, baseY = 0.2;
  const cx = (x0 + x1) / 2, cz = -6;
  const group = new THREE.Group();
  worldRoot.add(group);
  const cream = material(0xfff3dc), red = material(0xc8102e), yellow = material(0xffc72c);
  const floor = box(group, x1 - x0 - 0.2, 0.06, z1 - z0 - 0.2, material(0xf3efe6), cx, baseY + 0.03, cz, true);
  void floor;
  const addCollision = (minX, maxX, minY, maxY, minZ, maxZ, tag = 'campero') => addWorldBox(minX, maxX, minY, maxY, minZ, maxZ, tag, true);
  const wallX = (z, fromX, toX) => {
	const width = toX - fromX, mid = (fromX + toX) / 2;
	box(group, width, 0.9, thickness, cream, mid, baseY + 0.45, z, true);
	box(group, width, height - 2.9, thickness, red, mid, baseY + 2.9 + (height - 2.9) / 2, z, true);
	addCollision(fromX, toX, baseY, baseY + height, z - thickness / 2, z + thickness / 2);
  };
  const wallZ = (x, fromZ, toZ) => {
	const depth = toZ - fromZ, mid = (fromZ + toZ) / 2;
	box(group, thickness, 0.9, depth, cream, x, baseY + 0.45, mid, true);
	box(group, thickness, height - 0.9, depth, red, x, baseY + 0.9 + (height - 0.9) / 2, mid, true);
	addCollision(x - thickness / 2, x + thickness / 2, baseY, baseY + height, fromZ, toZ);
  };
  wallX(z0 + thickness / 2, x0, x1);
  wallX(z1 - thickness / 2, x0, x1);
  wallZ(x0 + thickness / 2, z0, z1);
  wallZ(x1 - thickness / 2, z0, cz - 1.3);
  wallZ(x1 - thickness / 2, cz + 1.3, z1);
  box(group, thickness, height - 0.9, 2.6, red, x1 - thickness / 2, baseY + 0.9 + (height - 0.9) / 2, cz, true);
  addCollision(x1 - thickness, x1, baseY + 2.9, baseY + height, cz - 1.3, cz + 1.3);
  box(group, 0.2, 2.9, 0.2, yellow, x1 - thickness / 2, baseY + 1.45, cz - 1.3);
  box(group, 0.2, 2.9, 0.2, yellow, x1 - thickness / 2, baseY + 1.45, cz + 1.3);
  box(group, x1 - x0 + 0.6, 0.3, z1 - z0 + 0.6, material(0x7f1d1d), cx, baseY + height + 0.15, cz, true);
  addCollision(x0 - 0.3, x1 + 0.3, baseY + height, baseY + height + 0.3, z0 - 0.3, z1 + 0.3, 'campero_roof');
  [-2.4, 2.4].forEach((dz) => box(group, 2.4, 0.05, 1, material(0xffffff, { emissive: 0xfff2cc, emissiveIntensity: 1.2 }), cx + 0.5, baseY + height - 0.05, cz + dz));
  const sign = new THREE.Group();
  sign.position.set(x1 + 0.12, baseY + height + 1.3, cz);
  sign.rotation.y = Math.PI / 2;
  createNeonSign(sign, 'POLLO CAMPERO', 0, 0, 0, '#ffc72c', '#b91c1c', 7.2, 1.5);
  group.add(sign);
  for (const z of [cz - 2.8, cz + 2.8]) box(group, 0.2, 0.9, 0.2, material(0xcbd5e1, { metalness: 0.8, roughness: 0.3 }), x1 + 0.12, baseY + height + 0.75, z);
  const chicken = new THREE.Group();
  chicken.position.set(cx + 1.5, baseY + height + 0.3, cz);
  chicken.rotation.y = Math.PI / 2;
  box(chicken, 0.6, 0.9, 0.6, material(0x7f1d1d), 0, 0.45, 0);
  sphere(chicken, 1, material(0xfafafa), 0, 1.9, 0, 1, 1, 1.25);
  sphere(chicken, 0.55, material(0xfafafa), 0, 2.95, 0.75);
  [-0.18, 0, 0.18].forEach((dx, i) => box(chicken, 0.15, 0.3 + (i === 1 ? 0.12 : 0), 0.15, material(0xdc2626), dx, 3.55 + (i === 1 ? 0.05 : 0), 0.75));
  box(chicken, 0.2, 0.14, 0.4, material(0xf59e0b), 0, 2.9, 1.25);
  box(chicken, 0.14, 0.3, 0.1, material(0xdc2626), 0, 2.62, 1.2);
  sphere(chicken, 0.08, material(0x0b0b0d), 0.28, 3.05, 1.15);
  sphere(chicken, 0.08, material(0x0b0b0d), -0.28, 3.05, 1.15);
  group.add(chicken);
  for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) box(group, 1.2, 0.1, 0.6, material(i % 2 ? 0xffc72c : 0xc8102e), x1 + 0.6, baseY + 3.15, cz - 1.8 + i * 0.6);
  box(group, 0.9, 1, 5.2, red, 51.3, baseY + 0.5, cz, true);
  box(group, 1.15, 0.08, 5.4, material(0xf5e6c8), 51.3, baseY + 1.04, cz);
  addCollision(50.8, 51.8, baseY, 1.5, cz - 2.6, cz + 2.6, 'campero_counter');
  createNeonSign(group, 'POLLO FRITO â€¢ COMBOS', x0 + thickness + 0.12, baseY + 2.7, cz, '#7f1d1d', '#ffd24a', 4.8, 0.9);
  const steel = material(0xcbd5e1, { metalness: 0.8, roughness: 0.3 });
  for (const [x, z] of [[55.2, -8.3], [57.8, -8.3], [55.2, -3.7], [57.8, -3.7]]) {
	cylinder(group, 0.55, 0.55, 0.06, red, x, baseY + 0.9, z, 14);
	cylinder(group, 0.07, 0.1, 0.88, steel, x, baseY + 0.44, z, 8);
	addCollision(x - 0.5, x + 0.5, baseY, baseY + 1.1, z - 0.5, z + 0.5, 'campero_table');
  }
  mapShapes.push({ x: cx, z: cz, w: x1 - x0, d: z1 - z0, color: '#facc15', border: '#b91c1c', enterable: false });
  enterableZones.push({ minX: x0 + 0.3, maxX: x1 - 0.3, minZ: z0 + 0.3, maxZ: z1 - 0.2, name: 'POLLO CAMPERO' });

  const home = (def) => addFestivalNpc({ ...def, gtHome: { x: def.x, z: def.z } }, 'campero');
  const man = (extra) => ({
	skin: choose(SKIN), hair: choose(HAIR), look: 'man', hat: choose(['cap', 'none', 'none']),
	shirt: choose([BLUE, WHITE, 0x16a34a, 0xf97316]), pants: choose([0x1e293b, 0x334155]), line: choose(CAMPERO_LINES), ...extra
  });
  const woman = (extra) => ({
	skin: choose(SKIN), hair: choose(HAIR), look: 'woman', skirt: choose([0x1d4ed8, 0x7f1d1d, 0x14532d, 0x6d28d9]),
	shirt: choose(PALETTE), pants: 0x1e293b, line: choose(CAMPERO_LINES), ...extra
  });
  home(woman({ name: 'CASHIER ROSA', x: 50.2, z: -7.4, yaw: Math.PI / 2, shirt: 0xc8102e, line: 'Welcome to Pollo Campero! What can I get you?' }));
  home(man({ name: 'FRY COOK LUIS', x: 50.2, z: -4.6, yaw: Math.PI / 2, shirt: 0xc8102e, hat: 'cap', line: 'Fresh out of the fryer! Be careful, it is hot.' }));
  home(man({ name: 'MARIO', x: 52.6, z: -7, yaw: -Math.PI / 2 }));
  home(woman({ name: 'LUCÃA', x: 52.6, z: -5.2, yaw: -Math.PI / 2 }));
  home(man({ name: 'ERICK', x: 54.25, z: -8.3, yaw: Math.PI / 2 }));
  home(woman({ name: 'CARMEN', x: 58.75, z: -8.3, yaw: -Math.PI / 2 }));
  home(man({ name: 'JOSÃ‰', x: 55.2, z: -4.65, yaw: 0 }));
  home(woman({ name: 'MIRIAM', x: 57.8, z: -4.65, yaw: 0 }));
  const queue = [[61.3, cz, -Math.PI / 2], [62.5, cz, -Math.PI / 2], [63.7, cz, -Math.PI / 2], [64.6, cz, -Math.PI / 2], [64.6, -7.2, 0], [64.6, -8.4, 0], [64.6, -9.6, 0], [64.6, -10.8, 0], [64.6, -12, 0]];
  queue.forEach(([x, z, yaw], i) => home((i % 2 ? woman : man)({ name: choose(i % 2 ? W_NAMES : M_NAMES), x, z, yaw })));
}

export function buildGuatemalanFestival() {
  if (festival.built) return;
  festival.built = true;
  for (let row = 0; row < 10; row++) for (let column = 0; column < 5; column++) {
	const woman = Math.random() < 0.45;
	const npc = addFestivalNpc({
	  name: choose(woman ? W_NAMES : M_NAMES), x: 0, z: 0, yaw: 0, scale: random(0.8, 0.89),
	  shirt: woman ? choose(PALETTE) : choose([BLUE, BLUE, WHITE, WHITE, 0xfacc15]),
	  pants: woman ? 0x1e293b : choose([0x1e293b, 0x334155, WHITE]), skin: choose(SKIN), hair: choose(HAIR),
	  look: woman ? 'woman' : 'man', skirt: choose([0x1d4ed8, 0x7f1d1d, 0x14532d, 0x6d28d9, 0x9a3412]),
	  hat: woman ? null : choose(['straw', 'cap', 'none', 'none']), sash: Math.random() < 0.3 ? BLUE : null,
	  gtRole: row % 3 === 0 && column % 2 === 0 ? 'flag' : null, line: choose(PARADE_LINES)
	}, 'parade');
	npc.gtRow = row;
	npc.gtColumn = column;
	npc.gtJitter = random(-0.35, 0.35);
	const slot = paradeSlot(npc);
	npc.x = npc.homeX = slot.x;
	npc.z = npc.homeZ = slot.z;
	npc.yaw = slot.yaw;
  }
  festival.floats.push(buildQuetzalFloat(), buildTikalFloat());
  buildBunting();
  buildCampero();
}

function placeFloat(group, distance) {
  const point = pathAt(distance);
  group.position.x = point.x;
  group.position.z = point.z;
  group.rotation.y = Math.atan2(point.hx, point.hz);
}

function busy(npc) {
  return npc.asleep || npc.hostile || npc.fleeTimer > 0 || npc.stunTimer > 0 || Math.abs(npc.kvx) + Math.abs(npc.kvz) > 0.3;
}

function animateWalk(npc, dt, fast) {
  const data = npc.mesh.userData;
  npc.walkCycle += dt * (fast ? 12 : 7.5);
  const swing = Math.sin(npc.walkCycle) * (fast ? 0.75 : 0.5);
  data.legL.rotation.x = swing;
  data.legR.rotation.x = -swing;
  data.armL.rotation.x = -swing * 0.75;
  data.armR.rotation.x = swing * 0.75;
  data.armL.rotation.z = 0;
  data.armR.rotation.z = 0;
}

export function updateGuatemalanFestival(dt, elapsed) {
  if (!festival.built) return;
  festival.lead += 2 * dt;
  placeFloat(festival.floats[0], festival.lead);
  placeFloat(festival.floats[1], festival.lead - 44);
  festival.musicians.forEach((musician, i) => {
	const data = musician.userData, swing = Math.sin(elapsed * 9 + i * 1.6);
	data.armL.rotation.x = -1.15 + swing * 0.45;
	data.armR.rotation.x = -1.15 - swing * 0.45;
  });
  for (const npc of festival.parade) {
	if (multiplayer.mode === 'guest' || busy(npc)) continue;
	const slot = paradeSlot(npc);
	const dx = slot.x - npc.x, dz = slot.z - npc.z, distance = Math.hypot(dx, dz);
	const walking = distance > 0.35;
	if (walking) {
	  const step = Math.min(distance, 6.2 * dt);
	  npc.x += dx / distance * step;
	  npc.z += dz / distance * step;
	  npc.yaw = Math.atan2(dx, dz);
	} else {
	  npc.x = slot.x;
	  npc.z = slot.z;
	  npc.yaw = slot.yaw;
	}
	npc.moving = walking;
	if (npc.mesh && npc.spawned) {
	  npc.mesh.position.set(npc.x, 0.18, npc.z);
	  npc.mesh.rotation.y = npc.yaw;
	  animateWalk(npc, dt, walking);
	  const data = npc.mesh.userData;
	  if (data.flag) {
		data.armR.rotation.x = -0.55;
		data.flag.rotation.y = Math.sin(elapsed * 4 + npc.id) * 0.35;
	  } else if (Math.sin(elapsed * 0.45 + npc.id * 1.9) > 0.82) {
		data.armL.rotation.x = -2.6 + Math.sin(elapsed * 9 + npc.id) * 0.35;
		data.armR.rotation.x = -2.6 + Math.cos(elapsed * 9 + npc.id) * 0.35;
	  }
	}
  }
  for (const npc of festival.homers) {
	if (multiplayer.mode === 'guest' || busy(npc) || !npc.mesh || !npc.spawned) continue;
	npc.x = npc.gtHome.x;
	npc.z = npc.gtHome.z;
	npc.yaw = npc.gtYaw;
	npc.mesh.position.set(npc.x, 0.18, npc.z);
	npc.mesh.rotation.y = npc.yaw;
  }
  festival.confettiTimer -= dt;
  if (festival.confettiTimer <= 0 && Math.hypot(player.x - festival.floats[0].position.x, player.z - festival.floats[0].position.z) < 90) {
	festival.confettiTimer = 0.3;
	const npc = choose(festival.parade);
	if (npc.mesh) spawnParticles(npc.x, 2.6, npc.z, choose([BLUE, WHITE, 0xfacc15, 0xdc2626, 0x16a34a, 0xf97316]), 4, 3.2);
	spawnParticles(festival.floats[0].position.x, 4.6, festival.floats[0].position.z, choose([0xfacc15, 0xdc2626, BLUE]), 3, 4);
  }
  festival.musicTimer -= dt;
  if (festival.musicTimer <= 0) {
	festival.musicTimer = 0.22;
	const distance = Math.hypot(player.x - festival.floats[0].position.x, player.z - festival.floats[0].position.z);
	if (distance < 75 && sfx.enabled && player.y > -1.2) {
	  const scale = [293.66, 329.63, 369.99, 440, 493.88, 587.33];
	  const pattern = [0, 2, 4, 2, 3, 2, 1, 2, 0, 2, 4, 5, 4, 3, 2, 1];
	  const frequency = scale[pattern[festival.musicStep % pattern.length]];
	  const volume = 0.07 * Math.pow(1 - distance / 75, 1.5);
	  sfx.tone(frequency, frequency * 0.985, 0.3, 'sine', volume);
	  sfx.tone(frequency * 3, frequency * 3, 0.1, 'sine', volume * 0.25);
	  if (festival.musicStep % 4 === 0) sfx.tone(frequency / 2, frequency / 2.02, 0.4, 'triangle', volume * 0.7);
	  festival.musicStep++;
	}
  }
}

export function decorateGuatemalanNpc(npc, mesh) {
  if (npc.gtFestival) decorateFestivalNpc(mesh, npc);
}

export function renderGuatemalanMap(ctx, canvas, centered) {
  if (!festival.built) return;
  const scale = centered ? 0.65 : 0.38;
  const refX = centered ? player.x : -29;
  const refZ = centered ? player.z : -190;
  const x = (wx) => canvas.width / 2 + (wx - refX) * scale;
  const y = (wz) => canvas.height / 2 + (wz - refZ) * scale;
  ctx.fillStyle = '#facc15';
  ctx.font = 'bold 9px sans-serif';
  ctx.fillText('POLLO CAMPERO', x(54.5), y(-6));
  if (player.y < -1.2) return;
  for (let i = 0; i < festival.parade.length; i += 3) {
	const npc = festival.parade[i];
	ctx.fillStyle = i % 2 ? '#f8fafc' : '#4997d0';
	ctx.fillRect(x(npc.x) - 1, y(npc.z) - 1, 2.4, 2.4);
  }
  for (const group of festival.floats) {
	ctx.fillStyle = '#facc15';
	ctx.fillRect(x(group.position.x) - 2.5, y(group.position.z) - 2.5, 5, 5);
  }
}

Object.assign(hooks, {
  decorateNpc: (mesh, npc) => decorateGuatemalanNpc(npc, mesh),
  drawMapOverlay: (ctx, canvas, centered) => renderGuatemalanMap(ctx, canvas, centered)
});


==================== FILE: js\input.js ====================
// =============================================================================
// input.js â€” Keyboard, mouse look, pointer lock, weapon wheel and in-game hotkeys (call initInput() once from main.js).
// =============================================================================
import { levelData } from './levelData.js';
import { MAX_HOTDOG_AMMO, aura, climb, flashEdge, gameState, hooks, inputState, keys, manholeOpen, meteorDrop, npcs, player, pushFeed, sausageRefillZones, under } from './state.js';
import { canvas } from './engine.js';
import { sfx } from './audio.js';
import { useLeftHandItem } from './items.js';
import { enterVehicle, exitCurrentCar, getNearestVehicle, shiftVehicleGear, vehicles } from './vehicles.js';
import { findMantleTarget, getNearestElevatorPad, nearestManhole, startClimb, syncEquippedMeshes, triggerRightHandAttack } from './player.js';

export function applyLookDelta(dx, dy) {
  const mult = 0.0023 * gameState.settings.sensitivity * (gameState.isAiming ? 0.55 : 1.0);
  if (gameState.inCar && gameState.thirdPerson) {
    const vehicle = vehicles[gameState.activeCarIndex];
    if (vehicle) {
      vehicle.cameraYaw -= dx * mult;
      vehicle.cameraPitch = Math.max(-0.2, Math.min(1.05, vehicle.cameraPitch - dy * mult));
      vehicle.cameraLookIdle = 0;
    }
    return;
  }
  player.yaw -= dx * mult;
  player.pitch = Math.max(-1.42, Math.min(1.42, player.pitch - dy * mult));
}
/** Attach all DOM / window listeners. Call once from main.js (never from Studio). */
export function initInput() {

document.addEventListener('pointerlockchange', () => {
  const locked = document.pointerLockElement === canvas;
  document.getElementById('lock-hint').style.display = locked ? 'none' : 'block';
});

window.addEventListener('contextmenu', (e) => {
  if (gameState.screen === 'playing') {
    e.preventDefault();
  }
});

canvas.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
  if (gameState.screen !== 'playing') return;
  if (gameState.inventoryOpen || gameState.mapOpen || gameState.settingsOpen) return;
  sfx.unlock();

  if (e.button === 2) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!gameState.inCar && !meteorDrop.active && gameState.activeRightWeapon === 'hotdog_shooter') {
      gameState.isAiming = !gameState.isAiming;
      sfx.uiClick();
      hooks.updateHUD();
    }
    return;
  }

  if ((!gameState.settings.mobileControls || navigator.maxTouchPoints === 0) && document.pointerLockElement !== canvas && canvas.requestPointerLock) {
    try {
      const res = canvas.requestPointerLock();
      if (res && res.catch) res.catch(() => {});
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  inputState.isDraggingLook = true;
  inputState.lastMouseX = e.clientX;
  inputState.lastMouseY = e.clientY;

  if (e.button === 0 && !meteorDrop.active) {
    inputState.isPrimaryFireHeld = true;
    if (gameState.inCar) sfx.horn();
    else triggerRightHandAttack();
  }
});

window.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
  if (gameState.screen !== 'playing') return;
  if (gameState.inventoryOpen || gameState.mapOpen || gameState.settingsOpen) return;

  if (document.pointerLockElement === canvas) {
    applyLookDelta(e.movementX || 0, e.movementY || 0);
  } else if (inputState.isDraggingLook) {
    applyLookDelta(e.clientX - inputState.lastMouseX, e.clientY - inputState.lastMouseY);
    inputState.lastMouseX = e.clientX;
    inputState.lastMouseY = e.clientY;
  }
});

window.addEventListener('mouseup', (e) => {
  if (e.button === 0) {
    inputState.isPrimaryFireHeld = false;
  }
  inputState.isDraggingLook = false;
});

window.addEventListener('wheel', (e) => {
  if (gameState.screen !== 'playing') return;
  if (gameState.inventoryOpen || gameState.mapOpen || gameState.settingsOpen || gameState.inCar || meteorDrop.active) return;
  hooks.cycleRightWeapon(e.deltaY > 0 ? 1 : -1);
}, { passive: true });

window.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
  const code = e.code;
  keys[code] = true;

  if (code === 'Tab' || code === 'Escape') {
    if (gameState.screen === 'playing') {
      e.preventDefault();
      if (gameState.settingsOpen) hooks.closeSettingsModal();
      else if (gameState.inventoryOpen) hooks.closeInventoryModal();
      else if (gameState.mapOpen) hooks.closeMapModal();
      else hooks.setScreen('paused');
      return;
    } else if (gameState.screen === 'paused') {
      e.preventDefault();
      if (gameState.settingsOpen) hooks.closeSettingsModal();
      else hooks.setScreen('playing');
      return;
    }
  }

  if (gameState.screen !== 'playing') return;
  sfx.unlock();

  if (code === 'KeyE') {
    if (gameState.mapOpen || gameState.settingsOpen) return;

    if (!gameState.inventoryOpen && !gameState.inCar && !meteorDrop.active) {
      for (const zone of sausageRefillZones) {
        if (Math.hypot(player.x - zone.x, player.z - zone.z) < 3.1 && Math.abs(player.y - zone.y) < 1.8) {
          if (!gameState.rightWeapons.includes('hotdog_shooter')) {
            gameState.rightWeapons.push('hotdog_shooter');
            gameState.activeRightWeapon = 'hotdog_shooter';
          }
          gameState.hotdogAmmo = MAX_HOTDOG_AMMO;
          sfx.pickup();
          flashEdge('rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.35)');
          pushFeed(`SAUSAGE HOPPER REFILLED (${MAX_HOTDOG_AMMO}/${MAX_HOTDOG_AMMO})!`, 'border-l-amber-400');
          syncEquippedMeshes();
          hooks.updateHUD();
          return;
        }
      }
    }

    if (!gameState.inventoryOpen && !gameState.inCar && !meteorDrop.active) {
      for (const npc of npcs) {
        if (!npc.spawned || npc.asleep) continue;
        if (Math.hypot(player.x - npc.x, player.z - npc.z) < 2.5 && Math.abs(player.y - 0.18) < 1.8) {
          const dlg = document.getElementById('npc-dialogue');
          document.getElementById('npc-dialogue-name').textContent = npc.name;
          document.getElementById('npc-dialogue-text').textContent = npc.line;
          dlg.style.display = 'block';
          gameState.dialogueTimer = 4.2;
          sfx.uiClick();
          return;
        }
      }
    }

    if (gameState.inventoryOpen) hooks.closeInventoryModal();
    else hooks.openInventoryModal();
    return;
  }

  if (code === 'KeyM') {
    if (gameState.mapOpen) hooks.closeMapModal();
    else hooks.openMapModal();
    return;
  }

  if (gameState.inventoryOpen || gameState.mapOpen || gameState.settingsOpen) return;

  if (code === 'KeyV') {
    gameState.thirdPerson = !gameState.thirdPerson;
    if (gameState.thirdPerson && !gameState.inCar) {
      gameState.isAiming = false;
    }
    sfx.uiClick();
    hooks.updateHUD();
  } else if (code === 'KeyQ') {
    hooks.cycleRightWeapon(1);
  } else if (code === 'KeyU') {
    if (!gameState.inCar && !meteorDrop.active) useLeftHandItem(gameState.activeLeftSlot);
  } else if (code === 'KeyC') {
    if (!gameState.inCar && !meteorDrop.active) {
      player.crouching = !player.crouching;
      sfx.uiClick();
    }
  } else if (code === 'Space') {
    if (gameState.inCar || player.mantle.active || meteorDrop.active) return;
    const mTarget = findMantleTarget();
    if (mTarget) {
      player.mantle.active = true;
      player.mantle.timer = 0;
      player.mantle.startX = player.x;
      player.mantle.startY = player.y;
      player.mantle.startZ = player.z;
      player.mantle.endX = mTarget.x;
      player.mantle.endY = mTarget.y;
      player.mantle.endZ = mTarget.z;
      player.vy = 0;
      player.grounded = false;
      player.crouching = false;
      sfx.mantle();
      pushFeed('MANTLED LEDGE', 'border-l-sky-400');
    } else if ((player.grounded || player.coyote > 0) && gameState.stamina >= 5) {
      player.vy = levelData.params.player.jumpForce;
      player.coyote = 0;
      player.grounded = false;
      player.peakAirY = player.y;
      gameState.stamina = Math.max(0, gameState.stamina - 5);
    }
  } else if (code === 'KeyI' && gameState.inCar) {
    shiftVehicleGear(1);
  } else if (code === 'KeyK' && gameState.inCar) {
    shiftVehicleGear(-1);
  } else if (code === 'KeyF') {
if (gameState.inCar) {
exitCurrentCar();
} else if (!climb.on) {
const u = under();
const mi = nearestManhole(u ? 3.2 : 2.4);
if (mi >= 0) {
if (u) { startClimb(1, mi); }
else if (!manholeOpen[mi]) { manholeOpen[mi] = true; sfx.mantle(); pushFeed('MANHOLE COVER OPENED â€” [F] TO CLIMB DOWN', 'border-l-amber-400'); }
else startClimb(-1, mi);
} else if (aura.ahF && aura.ahHint && aura.ahHint()) {
aura.ahF();
} else {
const elev = getNearestElevatorPad();
if (elev) {
player.x = elev.dest.x;
player.y = elev.dest.y;
player.z = elev.dest.z;
player.vy = 0;
player.grounded = true;
player.peakAirY = elev.dest.y;
sfx.elevatorDing();
flashEdge('rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.35)');
pushFeed(
elev.toRoof ? `ROOFTOP HELIPAD â€” ${elev.sk.name}` : `GROUND LOBBY â€” ${elev.sk.name}`,
'border-l-sky-400'
);
hooks.updateHUD();
} else {
const near = getNearestVehicle(4.2);
if (near) enterVehicle(near.idx);
}
}
}
} else if (['Digit1', 'Digit2', 'Digit3', 'Digit4', 'Digit5'].includes(code)) {
    gameState.activeLeftSlot = Number(code.replace('Digit', '')) - 1;
    syncEquippedMeshes();
    hooks.updateHUD();
  }
});

window.addEventListener('keyup', (e) => {
  keys[e.code] = false;
});

window.addEventListener('blur', () => {
  for (const code of Object.keys(keys)) keys[code] = false;
  inputState.isDraggingLook = false;
  inputState.isPrimaryFireHeld = false;
});
}

export const crateMat = makeMat(0xc2853e, { texType: 'wood', repeatX: 1, repeatY: 1, bumpScale: 0.02 });
export const barrelMat = makeMat(0xb91c1c, { texType: 'corrugated', repeatX: 3, repeatY: 1, metalness: 0.80, roughness: 0.32 });
crateMat.userData.shared = true;
barrelMat.userData.shared = true;

export const partGeo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.16, 0.16, 0.16), partMats = {};

export function freeGeo(o) {
if (!o) return;
const seenGeo = new Set(), seenMat = new Set();
o.traverse((c) => {
if (c.geometry && !c.geometry.userData.shared && !seenGeo.has(c.geometry)) {
seenGeo.add(c.geometry);
c.geometry.dispose();
}
const mats = Array.isArray(c.material) ? c.material : (c.material ? [c.material] : []);
for (const m of mats) {
if (m && !m.userData.shared && !seenMat.has(m)) { seenMat.add(m); m.dispose(); }
}
});
}

export function spawnParticles(x, y, z, colorHex, count = 10, speed = 5.5) {
  const mat = partMats[colorHex] || (partMats[colorHex] = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: colorHex }));
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const m = new THREE.Mesh(partGeo, mat);
    m.position.set(x, y, z);
    worldRoot.add(m);
    particleBursts.push({
      mesh: m,
      vx: (Math.random() - 0.5) * speed,
      vy: Math.random() * speed * 0.8 + 1.5,
      vz: (Math.random() - 0.5) * speed,
      life: 0.55 + Math.random() * 0.3
    });
  }
}

export function buildPickupMesh(type, id) {
  const g = new THREE.Group();

  let color = 0xf59e0b;
  let mesh;
  if (type === 'right') {
    if (id === 'bat') {
      mesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.055, 0.032, 0.9, 8), makeMat(0xd97706, { texType: 'wood' }));
      mesh.rotation.z = 0.5;
      color = 0xd97706;
    } else if (id === 'crowbar') {
      mesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.08, 0.85, 0.08), makeMat(0xef4444, { metalness: 0.85 }));
      mesh.rotation.z = 0.45;
      color = 0xef4444;
    } else if (id === 'pipe') {
      mesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.05, 0.05, 0.85, 8), makeMat(0x94a3b8, { texType: 'brushedsteel', metalness: 0.9 }));
      mesh.rotation.z = 0.45;
      color = 0x94a3b8;
    } else if (id === 'hotdog_shooter') {
      const hdGun = new THREE.Group();
      const bunBody = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CapsuleGeometry(0.11, 0.52, 6, 8), makeMat(0xd97706));
      bunBody.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
      const barrel = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.07, 0.08, 0.65, 10), makeMat(0xdc2626, { metalness: 0.7 }));
      barrel.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
      barrel.position.y = 0.06;
      const tip = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.065, 8, 6), makeMat(0xb91c1c));
      tip.position.set(0, 0.06, 0.34);
      hdGun.add(bunBody, barrel, tip);
      mesh = hdGun;
      color = 0xfacc15;
    }
  } else {
    const itemDef = LEFT_ITEMS[id] || LEFT_ITEMS.cash;
    color = new THREE.Color(itemDef.color).getHex();
    mesh = new THREE.Mesh(
      new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.42, 0.28, 0.32),
      makeMat(color, { emissive: color, emissiveIntensity: 0.3 })
    );
  }

  const ring = new THREE.Mesh(
    new THREE.RingGeometry(0.45, 0.58, 16),
    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color, side: THREE.DoubleSide })
  );
  ring.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
  ring.position.y = -0.50;

  g.add(mesh, ring);
  g.userData.pickupColor = color;
  return g;
}

export function spawnWorldPickup(type, id, x, y, z, opts = {}) {
  const g = buildPickupMesh(type, id);
  g.position.set(x, y + 0.55, z);
  if (opts.rot) g.rotation.set(opts.rot[0], opts.rot[1], opts.rot[2]);
  if (opts.scale) g.scale.set(opts.scale[0], opts.scale[1], opts.scale[2]);
  if (opts.decorate) opts.decorate(g);
  worldRoot.add(g);
  const rec = { type, id, x, y, z, group: g, baseY: y + 0.55, phase: Math.random() * 6 };
  worldPickups.push(rec);
  return rec;
}

// Smashable wooden crate. pos = floor point under the crate; rot/scale optional (Studio entities).
export function spawnSmashCrate(x, y, z, loot = null, opts = {}) {
  const holder = new THREE.Group();
  holder.position.set(x, y, z);
  const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.05, 1.05, 1.05), crateMat);
  mesh.position.y = 0.525;
  mesh.castShadow = true;
  mesh.receiveShadow = true;
  holder.add(mesh);
  if (opts.rot) holder.rotation.set(opts.rot[0], opts.rot[1], opts.rot[2]);
  if (opts.scale) holder.scale.set(opts.scale[0], opts.scale[1], opts.scale[2]);
  if (opts.decorate) opts.decorate(holder);
  worldRoot.add(holder);
  const b = uprightFootprint(holder, 0.53, 0.53, 1.05);
  const box = addWorldBox(b.minX, b.maxX, y, y + b.height, b.minZ, b.maxZ, 'crate', true);
  const rec = { x, y, z, hp: 35, mesh: holder, box, loot };
  smashableCrates.push(rec);
  return rec;
}

// Explosive hazard barrel. Same pos/rot/scale convention as crates.
export function spawnHazardBarrel(x, y, z, opts = {}) {
  const holder = new THREE.Group();
  holder.position.set(x, y, z);
  const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.42, 0.42, 1.15, 10), barrelMat);
  mesh.position.y = 0.575;
  mesh.castShadow = true;
  holder.add(mesh);
  if (opts.rot) holder.rotation.set(opts.rot[0], opts.rot[1], opts.rot[2]);
  if (opts.scale) holder.scale.set(opts.scale[0], opts.scale[1], opts.scale[2]);
  if (opts.decorate) opts.decorate(holder);
  worldRoot.add(holder);
  const b = uprightFootprint(holder, 0.44, 0.44, 1.15);
  const box = addWorldBox(b.minX, b.maxX, y, y + b.height, b.minZ, b.maxZ, 'barrel', true);
  const rec = { x, y, z, hp: 30, mesh: holder, box };
  hazardBarrels.push(rec);
  return rec;
}

export function buildRightWeaponMesh(id) {
  const g = new THREE.Group();
  if (id === 'bat') {
    const handle = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.024, 0.032, 0.24, 8), makeMat(0x1e293b));
    handle.position.y = 0.08;
    const barrel = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.048, 0.026, 0.56, 8), makeMat(0xd97706, { texType: 'wood' }));
    barrel.position.y = 0.46;
    g.add(handle, barrel);
    g.rotation.x = -0.32;
  } else if (id === 'crowbar') {
    const shaft = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.024, 0.024, 0.64, 6), makeMat(0xb91c1c, { metalness: 0.85 }));
    shaft.position.y = 0.28;
    const hook = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.05, 0.05, 0.18), makeMat(0xdbeafe, { metalness: 0.9 }));
    hook.position.set(0, 0.60, -0.07);
    g.add(shaft, hook);
    g.rotation.x = -0.32;
  } else if (id === 'pipe') {
    const pipe = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.034, 0.034, 0.66, 8), makeMat(0x94a3b8, { texType: 'brushedsteel', metalness: 0.92 }));
    pipe.position.y = 0.30;
    const flange = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.048, 0.048, 0.08, 8), makeMat(0x475569, { metalness: 0.9 }));
    flange.position.y = 0.60;
    g.add(pipe, flange);
    g.rotation.x = -0.32;
  } else if (id === 'hotdog_shooter') {
    const bunMat = makeMat(0xd97706, { roughness: 0.58 });
    const redMat = makeMat(0xdc2626, { metalness: 0.72, roughness: 0.26 });
    const yellowMat = makeMat(0xfacc15, { roughness: 0.35 });
    const steelMat = makeMat(0xe2e8f0, { metalness: 0.92, roughness: 0.18 });

    const bunL = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CapsuleGeometry(0.068, 0.36, 6, 8), bunMat);
    bunL.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
    bunL.position.set(-0.042, 0.02, -0.14);

    const bunR = bunL.clone();
    bunR.position.x = 0.042;

    const barrel = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.052, 0.062, 0.54, 10), redMat);
    barrel.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
    barrel.position.set(0, 0.055, -0.20);

    const mustardStripe = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.025, 0.018, 0.48), yellowMat);
    mustardStripe.position.set(0, 0.112, -0.20);

    const muzzle = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.064, 0.064, 0.06, 12), steelMat);
    muzzle.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
    muzzle.position.set(0, 0.055, -0.47);

    const loadedTip = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.048, 8, 6), makeMat(0xb91c1c));
    loadedTip.position.set(0, 0.055, -0.49);

    const scope = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.024, 0.024, 0.22, 8), makeMat(0x0f172a, { metalness: 0.85 }));
    scope.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
    scope.position.set(0, 0.145, -0.14);

    g.add(bunL, bunR, barrel, mustardStripe, muzzle, loadedTip, scope);
  }
  return g;
}

export function buildLeftItemMesh(id) {
  const g = new THREE.Group();
  if (id === 'medkit') {
    const box = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.18, 0.14, 0.09), makeMat(0xf8fafc));
    const c1 = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.08, 0.026, 0.10), makeMat(0xef4444));
    const c2 = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.026, 0.08, 0.10), makeMat(0xef4444));
    g.add(box, c1, c2);
  } else if (id === 'soda') {
    const can = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.042, 0.042, 0.14, 10), makeMat(0x0284c7, { metalness: 0.85 }));
    const rim = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.044, 0.044, 0.02, 10), makeMat(0xe2e8f0, { metalness: 0.9 }));
    rim.position.y = 0.065;
    g.add(can, rim);
  } else if (id === 'burger') {
    const bunTop = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.055, 0.072, 0.045, 10), makeMat(0xd97706));
    bunTop.position.y = 0.032;
    const patty = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.074, 0.074, 0.028, 10), makeMat(0x451a03));
    const bunBot = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.07, 0.065, 0.03, 10), makeMat(0xd97706));
    bunBot.position.y = -0.028;
    g.add(bunTop, patty, bunBot);
  } else if (id === 'flashlight') {
    const body = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.028, 0.028, 0.22, 8), makeMat(0x1e293b, { metalness: 0.8 }));
    body.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
    const lens = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.038, 0.032, 0.05, 8), makeMat(0xfef08a, { emissive: 0xfacc15, emissiveIntensity: 0.6 }));
    lens.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
    lens.position.z = -0.12;
    g.add(body, lens);
  } else if (id === 'cash') {
    const stack = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.16, 0.05, 0.09), makeMat(0x10b981));
    const band = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.03, 0.054, 0.094), makeMat(0xfef3c7));
    g.add(stack, band);
  }
  return g;
}

export function fireBallisticWiener() {
  if (gameState.hotdogAmmo <= 0) {
    sfx.uiClick();
    pushFeed('OUT OF SAUSAGES! REFILL AT AURA HOTDOGS OR PLAZA CART', 'border-l-rose-500');
    return;
  }
  gameState.hotdogAmmo--;
  sfx.wienerShot();

  const dir = new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, -1).applyQuaternion(camera.quaternion).normalize();
  const muzzlePos = new THREE.Vector3();
  if (gameState.thirdPerson || gameState.isAiming) {
    muzzlePos.set(player.x, player.y + 1.48, player.z).addScaledVector(dir, 0.9);
  } else {
    hooks.getRightMountWorldPosition(muzzlePos);
    muzzlePos.addScaledVector(dir, 0.45);
  }

  const wGroup = new THREE.Group();
  wGroup.position.copy(muzzlePos);
  const wienerMesh = new THREE.Mesh(
    new THREE.CapsuleGeometry(0.068, 0.34, 5, 8),
    makeMat(0xb91c1c, { roughness: 0.32 })
  );
  wienerMesh.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
  const mustardLine = new THREE.Mesh(
    new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.024, 0.02, 0.32),
    makeMat(0xfacc15)
  );
  mustardLine.position.y = 0.068;
  wGroup.add(wienerMesh, mustardLine);
  worldRoot.add(wGroup);

  const launchSpeed = gameState.isAiming ? 68.0 : 54.0;
  const vx = dir.x * launchSpeed;
  const vy = dir.y * launchSpeed + 2.8;
  const vz = dir.z * launchSpeed;

  projectileWieners.push({
    mesh: wGroup,
    x: muzzlePos.x,
    y: muzzlePos.y,
    z: muzzlePos.z,
    vx,
    vy,
    vz,
    life: 3.2
  });

  hooks.updateHUD();
}

export function updateProjectileWieners(dt) {
  const GRAVITY = 15.8;

  for (let i = projectileWieners.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const w = projectileWieners[i];
    w.life -= dt;
    w.vy -= GRAVITY * dt;
    w.x += w.vx * dt;
    w.y += w.vy * dt;
    w.z += w.vz * dt;

    w.mesh.position.set(w.x, w.y, w.z);
    w.mesh.lookAt(w.x + w.vx, w.y + w.vy, w.z + w.vz);

    let impacted = false;

    for (let n = 0; n < npcs.length; n++) {
const npc = npcs[n];
if (!npc.spawned || npc.asleep) continue;
      if (Math.hypot(w.x - npc.x, w.z - npc.z) < 0.72 && w.y >= 0.18 && w.y <= 1.95) {
        hooks.damageNpc(npc, 48, Math.atan2(w.vx, w.vz));
        sfx.wienerSplat();
        spawnParticles(w.x, w.y, w.z, 0xb91c1c, 8, 5.5);
        spawnParticles(w.x, w.y, w.z, 0xfacc15, 5, 4.5);
        if (!npc.asleep) {
          pushFeed(`WIENER HIT ${npc.name}!`, 'border-l-amber-400');
        }
        impacted = true;
        break;
      }
    }

    if (!impacted) {
      for (let c = smashableCrates.length - 1; c >= 0; c--) {
        const cr = smashableCrates[c];
        if (Math.abs(w.x - cr.x) < 0.72 && Math.abs(w.z - cr.z) < 0.72 && w.y >= cr.y && w.y <= cr.y + 1.15) {
          sfx.smash();
          spawnParticles(cr.x, cr.y + 0.55, cr.z, 0xd97706, 12, 6.5);
          worldRoot.remove(cr.mesh);
          freeGeo(cr.mesh);
          const wbIdx = worldBoxes.indexOf(cr.box);
          if (wbIdx !== -1) worldBoxes.splice(wbIdx, 1);
          if (cr.loot) spawnWorldPickup(cr.loot.type, cr.loot.id, cr.x, cr.y, cr.z);
          smashableCrates.splice(c, 1);
          pushFeed('CRATE SMASHED BY WIENER!', 'border-l-amber-400');
          impacted = true;
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    if (!impacted) {
      for (let b = hazardBarrels.length - 1; b >= 0; b--) {
        const br = hazardBarrels[b];
        if (Math.hypot(w.x - br.x, w.z - br.z) < 0.65 && w.y >= br.y && w.y <= br.y + 1.25) {
          sfx.smash();
          spawnParticles(br.x, br.y + 0.6, br.z, 0xf97316, 18, 9.0);
          worldRoot.remove(br.mesh);
          freeGeo(br.mesh);
          const wbIdx = worldBoxes.indexOf(br.box);
          if (wbIdx !== -1) worldBoxes.splice(wbIdx, 1);
          hazardBarrels.splice(b, 1);
          pushFeed('HAZARD BARREL DETONATED!', 'border-l-orange-500');
          impacted = true;
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    if (!impacted) {
      for (const lp of lampPosts) {
        if (lp.state === 'standing' && Math.hypot(w.x - lp.x, w.z - lp.z) < 0.45 && w.y >= 0.18 && w.y <= 4.6) {
          hooks.knockOverLamppost(lp, Math.atan2(w.vx, w.vz), 8.5);
          impacted = true;
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    if (!impacted) {
      if (w.y <= 0.18) {
        impacted = true;
        sfx.wienerSplat();
        spawnParticles(w.x, 0.24, w.z, 0xb91c1c, 6, 4.2);
        spawnParticles(w.x, 0.24, w.z, 0xfacc15, 4, 3.5);
      } else {
        for (let k = 0; k < worldBoxes.length; k++) {
          const wb = worldBoxes[k];
          if (w.x >= wb.minX && w.x <= wb.maxX && w.z >= wb.minZ && w.z <= wb.maxZ && w.y >= wb.minY && w.y <= wb.maxY) {
            impacted = true;
            sfx.wienerSplat();
            spawnParticles(w.x, w.y, w.z, 0xb91c1c, 6, 4.2);
            spawnParticles(w.x, w.y, w.z, 0xfacc15, 4, 3.5);
            break;
          }
        }
      }
    }

    if (impacted || w.life <= 0) {
      worldRoot.remove(w.mesh);
      freeGeo(w.mesh);
      projectileWieners.splice(i, 1);
    }
  }
}

export function useLeftHandItem(slotIndex = gameState.activeLeftSlot) {
  const slot = gameState.leftHotbar[slotIndex];
  if (!slot) return;
  const item = LEFT_ITEMS[slot.id];
  if (!item) return;

  player.leftUseTimer = 0.32;

  if (item.id === 'flashlight') {
    gameState.flashlightOn = !gameState.flashlightOn;
    sfx.uiClick();
    hooks.syncEquippedMeshes();
    pushFeed(`FLASHLIGHT ${gameState.flashlightOn ? 'ON' : 'OFF'}`, 'border-l-yellow-400');
    return;
  }

  if (item.id === 'medkit') {
    if (gameState.hp >= gameState.maxHp) {
      pushFeed('HEALTH ALREADY FULL', 'border-l-slate-400');
      return;
    }
    gameState.hp = Math.min(gameState.maxHp, gameState.hp + 40);
    sfx.heal();
    flashEdge('rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.35)');
    pushFeed('USED MEDKIT (+40 HEALTH)', 'border-l-emerald-400');
  } else if (item.id === 'soda') {
    gameState.stamina = Math.min(100, gameState.stamina + 65);
    gameState.speedBoostTimer = 7.5;
    sfx.heal();
    flashEdge('rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.35)');
    pushFeed('DRANK ENERGY DRINK (+STAMINA & SPEED)', 'border-l-sky-400');
  } else if (item.id === 'burger') {
    gameState.hp = Math.min(gameState.maxHp, gameState.hp + 20);
    gameState.stamina = Math.min(100, gameState.stamina + 20);
    sfx.heal();
    flashEdge('rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.32)');
    pushFeed('ATE STREET BURGER (+20 HP / +20 STAM)', 'border-l-amber-400');
  } else if (item.id === 'cash') {
    gameState.cash += 100;
    sfx.pickup();
    flashEdge('rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.32)');
    pushFeed('DEPOSITED +$100 CASH', 'border-l-emerald-400');
  }

  slot.count -= 1;
  if (slot.count <= 0) {
    gameState.leftHotbar[slotIndex] = null;
  }
  hooks.syncEquippedMeshes();
  hooks.updateHUD();
  if (gameState.inventoryOpen) hooks.renderInventoryModal();
}

export function addLeftItemToBag(id, qty = 1) {
  for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
    if (gameState.leftHotbar[i] && gameState.leftHotbar[i].id === id) {
      gameState.leftHotbar[i].count += qty;
      return true;
    }
  }
  for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
    if (!gameState.leftHotbar[i]) {
      gameState.leftHotbar[i] = { id, count: qty };
      return true;
    }
  }
  return false;
}

export function dropLeftSlot(slotIndex) {
  const slot = gameState.leftHotbar[slotIndex];
  if (!slot) return;
  const fwdX = -Math.sin(player.yaw);
  const fwdZ = -Math.cos(player.yaw);
  spawnWorldPickup('left', slot.id, player.x + fwdX * 1.4, player.y, player.z + fwdZ * 1.4);
  slot.count -= 1;
  if (slot.count <= 0) gameState.leftHotbar[slotIndex] = null;
  sfx.uiClick();
  hooks.syncEquippedMeshes();
  hooks.updateHUD();
  if (gameState.inventoryOpen) hooks.renderInventoryModal();
}
// Pure visual builders (no registration) used by Studio previews and entity tabs.
export function buildCrateMesh() {
  const holder = new THREE.Group();
  const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.05, 1.05, 1.05), crateMat);
  mesh.position.y = 0.525;
  mesh.castShadow = true;
  mesh.receiveShadow = true;
  holder.add(mesh);
  return holder;
}

export function buildBarrelMesh() {
  const holder = new THREE.Group();
  const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.42, 0.42, 1.15, 10), barrelMat);
  mesh.position.y = 0.575;
  mesh.castShadow = true;
  holder.add(mesh);
  return holder;
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Entity type catalogue (used by the world spawner and by Studio's Toolbox).
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
export const ENTITY_TYPES = ['vehicle', 'pickup', 'crate', 'barrel', 'ramp', 'block', 'prop', 'building', 'road'];

/** Create a fresh entity of the given prefab with a unique id. */
export function makeEntity(prefab, pos = [0, 0, 0], rot = [0, 0, 0]) {
  const base = { id: '', type: prefab.type, name: prefab.name, pos: [...pos], rot: [...rot], scale: [...(prefab.scale || [1, 1, 1])], texture: '', texRepeat: [1, 1], color: prefab.color || '' };
  const extra = structuredClone(prefab.extra || {});
  return Object.assign(base, extra);
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Load / save helpers
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
function isPlainObject(v) { return v && typeof v === 'object' && !Array.isArray(v); }

/** Deep-merge saved data over defaults so older saves keep working. */
function mergeDefaults(def, saved) {
  if (Array.isArray(def)) return Array.isArray(saved) ? structuredClone(saved) : structuredClone(def);
  if (!isPlainObject(def)) return saved === undefined ? def : saved;
  const out = {};
  for (const k of Object.keys(def)) out[k] = mergeDefaults(def[k], isPlainObject(saved) ? saved[k] : undefined);
  if (isPlainObject(saved)) for (const k of Object.keys(saved)) if (!(k in out)) out[k] = structuredClone(saved[k]);
  return out;
}

/** Reads localStorage override if valid JSON exists, else a clone of the defaults. */
export function loadLevelData() {
  try {
    const raw = typeof localStorage !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem(LEVEL_STORAGE_KEY) : null;
    if (raw) {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
      if (isPlainObject(parsed) && isPlainObject(parsed.params) && Array.isArray(parsed.entities)) {
        const merged = mergeDefaults(DEFAULT_LEVEL_DATA, parsed);
        merged.entities = structuredClone(parsed.entities);
        return merged;
      }
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('[levelData] ignoring invalid saved level:', err);
  }
  return structuredClone(DEFAULT_LEVEL_DATA);
}

/** Live singleton shared by every module (mutated in place by Studio). */
export const levelData = loadLevelData();

const TERRAIN_CELL_SIZE = 2;

function terrainCell(heights, x, z) {
  return Number(heights[`${x},${z}`]) || 0;
}

/** Bilinearly sample the saved terrain height offset at world coordinates. */
export function terrainHeightOffset(x, z, data = levelData) {
  const heights = data.terrain && data.terrain.heights;
  if (!heights) return 0;
  const gx = x / TERRAIN_CELL_SIZE, gz = z / TERRAIN_CELL_SIZE;
  const x0 = Math.floor(gx), z0 = Math.floor(gz);
  const tx = gx - x0, tz = gz - z0;
  const a = terrainCell(heights, x0, z0), b = terrainCell(heights, x0 + 1, z0);
  const c = terrainCell(heights, x0, z0 + 1), d = terrainCell(heights, x0 + 1, z0 + 1);
  return (a + (b - a) * tx) * (1 - tz) + (c + (d - c) * tx) * tz;
}

/** Raise, lower, or smooth the saved terrain heightfield with a soft circular brush. */
export function sculptTerrain(data, centerX, centerZ, radius, strength, mode = 'raise', baseHeightAt = () => 0) {
  if (!data.terrain || typeof data.terrain !== 'object') data.terrain = { cellSize: TERRAIN_CELL_SIZE, heights: {} };
  if (!data.terrain.heights || typeof data.terrain.heights !== 'object') data.terrain.heights = {};
  const heights = data.terrain.heights;
  const minX = Math.floor((centerX - radius) / TERRAIN_CELL_SIZE);
  const maxX = Math.ceil((centerX + radius) / TERRAIN_CELL_SIZE);
  const minZ = Math.floor((centerZ - radius) / TERRAIN_CELL_SIZE);
  const maxZ = Math.ceil((centerZ + radius) / TERRAIN_CELL_SIZE);
  const before = mode === 'smooth' ? { ...heights } : heights;
  const read = (x, z) => terrainCell(before, x, z);
  for (let iz = minZ; iz <= maxZ; iz++) {
    for (let ix = minX; ix <= maxX; ix++) {
      const distance = Math.hypot(ix * TERRAIN_CELL_SIZE - centerX, iz * TERRAIN_CELL_SIZE - centerZ);
      if (distance >= radius) continue;
      const t = 1 - distance / radius;
      const falloff = t * t * (3 - 2 * t);
      const key = `${ix},${iz}`;
      const current = read(ix, iz);
      let next;
      if (mode === 'smooth') {
        let total = 0;
        for (let dz = -1; dz <= 1; dz++) {
          for (let dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) {
            const sx = (ix + dx) * TERRAIN_CELL_SIZE, sz = (iz + dz) * TERRAIN_CELL_SIZE;
            total += baseHeightAt(sx, sz) + read(ix + dx, iz + dz);
          }
        }
        const currentHeight = baseHeightAt(ix * TERRAIN_CELL_SIZE, iz * TERRAIN_CELL_SIZE) + current;
        next = current + (total / 9 - currentHeight) * Math.min(1, strength * falloff);
      } else {
        next = current + (mode === 'lower' ? -1 : 1) * strength * falloff;
      }
      next = Math.round(next * 1000) / 1000;
      if (Math.abs(next) < 0.001) delete heights[key];
      else heights[key] = next;
    }
  }
}

/** Replace the contents of the live singleton while keeping its identity. */
export function replaceLevelData(next) {
  for (const k of Object.keys(levelData)) delete levelData[k];
  Object.assign(levelData, structuredClone(next));
  return levelData;
}

export function saveLevelData(data = levelData) {
  localStorage.setItem(LEVEL_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(data));
}

export function clearSavedLevelData() {
  localStorage.removeItem(LEVEL_STORAGE_KEY);
}

/** "#rrggbb" | number -> number (0xrrggbb). Empty/invalid -> fallback. */
export function colorToHex(c, fallback = 0xffffff) {
  if (typeof c === 'number') return c;
  if (typeof c === 'string' && /^#?[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(c.trim())) return parseInt(c.trim().replace('#', ''), 16);
  return fallback;
}

/** number -> "#rrggbb" */
export function hexToCss(n) {
  return '#' + (Math.max(0, Math.min(0xffffff, n | 0))).toString(16).padStart(6, '0');
}

let _idCounter = 0;
/** Unique entity id inside the given data set. */
export function nextEntityId(prefix, data = levelData) {
  const used = new Set(data.entities.map((e) => e.id));
  let id;
  do { _idCounter++; id = `${prefix}_${Date.now().toString(36)}${_idCounter}`; } while (used.has(id));
  return id;
}


==================== FILE: js\main.js ====================
// =============================================================================
// main.js â€” GAME entry point (index.html).
// Boots the engine, loads levelData, builds the static world + data-driven entities,
// spawns NPCs, wires input/UI and runs the gameplay animation loop.
// Top of the import hierarchy: nothing imports this file.
// =============================================================================
import { levelData } from './levelData.js';
import { initEngine, scene, camera, renderer, skyGroup, applyGraphicsTier, resizeRenderer, animatedWaterTex, updateSunLensFlare } from './engine.js';
import { preloadBoundAudio, preloadRegisteredTextures } from './assets.js';
import { sfx } from './audio.js';
import { gameState, hooks } from './state.js';
import { buildWorld } from './world.js';
import { buildSewerRats } from './npcs.js';
import { syncEquippedMeshes, applyLevelParams } from './player.js';
import { initInput } from './input.js';
import { initUI, updateHUD, renderMapCanvas } from './ui.js';
import { initMultiplayer, sendMultiplayerState, updateRemotePlayers } from './multiplayer.js';
import { initMobileControls } from './mobile.js';
import { buildGuatemalanFestival, updateGuatemalanFestival } from './guatemala.js';
import { buildCityTraffic, updateCityTraffic } from './traffic.js';
import { initAircraft, updateAircraft } from './aircraft.js';

let lastTime = performance.now();
let minimapTimer = 0;
let windowFocused = true;

function animate(now) {
  requestAnimationFrame(animate);
  const dt = Math.min(0.05, (now - lastTime) / 1000);
  lastTime = now;
  const elapsed = now * 0.001;
  hooks.syncMobileControls();

  if (animatedWaterTex) {
    animatedWaterTex.offset.x = (elapsed * 0.08) % 1;
    animatedWaterTex.offset.y = (elapsed * 0.05) % 1;
  }

  if (gameState.screen === 'start') {
    const ang = elapsed * 0.16;
    camera.position.set(Math.cos(ang) * 76, 36 + Math.sin(elapsed * 0.4) * 3, Math.sin(ang) * 76 - 24);
    camera.lookAt(0, 6, -32);
  } else if (gameState.screen === 'playing' && windowFocused && !document.hidden) {
    updateAircraft(dt, elapsed);
    updateCityTraffic(dt);
    updateGuatemalanFestival(dt, elapsed);
    minimapTimer += dt;
    if (minimapTimer >= 0.05) {
      minimapTimer = 0;
      renderMapCanvas(document.getElementById('minimap-canvas'), true);
    }
    updateRemotePlayers(dt);
    sendMultiplayerState(performance.now());
  }

  sfx.updateMusic();
  skyGroup.position.copy(camera.position);
  for (let i = 2; i < skyGroup.children.length; i++) {
    const cloud = skyGroup.children[i];
    cloud.position.x += dt * 0.3;
    if (cloud.position.x > 260) cloud.position.x = -260;
  }
  updateSunLensFlare();
  renderer.render(scene, camera);
}

async function updateLoadingProgress(percent, status) {
  const progress = document.getElementById('loading-progress');
  const percentLabel = document.getElementById('loading-percent');
  const statusLabel = document.getElementById('loading-status');
  const value = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round(percent)));
  if (progress) progress.style.width = `${value}%`;
  if (percentLabel) percentLabel.textContent = `${value}%`;
  if (statusLabel) statusLabel.textContent = status;
  await new Promise((resolve) => requestAnimationFrame(resolve));
}

async function boot() {
  const loadingStarted = performance.now();
  const loadingScreen = document.getElementById('loading-screen');
  await updateLoadingProgress(5, 'Initializing graphics');
  await initEngine(); // full-screen canvas (#game-canvas)
  await updateLoadingProgress(18, 'Loading game data');
  applyLevelParams(levelData);
  preloadRegisteredTextures(levelData);
  preloadBoundAudio(levelData);
  await updateLoadingProgress(30, 'Building Sunnyvale');

  // World: static city/freeway/desert/biomes -> levelData.entities -> Aura Hills overhaul
  buildWorld(levelData, {
    register: true,
    afterStatic: () => applyGraphicsTier('medium')
  });
  await updateLoadingProgress(72, 'Sunnyvale built');
  buildGuatemalanFestival();
  buildCityTraffic();
  initAircraft();
  buildSewerRats();
  await updateLoadingProgress(84, 'Preparing game systems');

  syncEquippedMeshes();
  initUI();
  initMultiplayer();
  initMobileControls();
  initInput();
  updateHUD();
  await updateLoadingProgress(96, 'Finalizing');

  window.addEventListener('blur', () => {
    windowFocused = false;
  });
  window.addEventListener('focus', () => {
    windowFocused = true;
    lastTime = performance.now();
  });

  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {
    lastTime = performance.now();
    if (document.hidden && sfx.ctx && sfx.ctx.state === 'running') sfx.ctx.suspend().catch(() => {});
    else if (!document.hidden && sfx.ctx && gameState.screen === 'playing') sfx.ctx.resume().catch(() => {});
  });

  window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
    camera.aspect = window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight;
    camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
    resizeRenderer();
  });

  requestAnimationFrame(animate);
  const minimumLoadingTime = 500;
  const remainingLoadingTime = minimumLoadingTime - (performance.now() - loadingStarted);
  if (remainingLoadingTime > 0) {
    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, remainingLoadingTime));
  }
  await updateLoadingProgress(100, 'Ready');
  loadingScreen.classList.add('is-hidden');
}

boot().catch((err) => {
  console.error(err);
  const loadingScreen = document.getElementById('loading-screen');
  if (loadingScreen) loadingScreen.classList.add('is-hidden');
  const banner = document.getElementById('error-banner');
  if (banner) {
    banner.textContent = `Initialization error: ${err.message}. Please refresh to retry.`;
    banner.style.display = 'block';
  }
});

const MOBILE_PREF_KEY = 'gtaAura.mobileControls.v1';
const heldKeys = new Map();
const activeActions = new Map();
let mobileDevice = false;
let root = null;
let lookPointer = null;
let lookX = 0;
let lookY = 0;
let stickPointer = null;
let lastSync = 0;

function setHeldKey(code, down, source) {
  let sources = heldKeys.get(code);
  if (!sources) { sources = new Set(); heldKeys.set(code, sources); }
  if (down) sources.add(source);
  else sources.delete(source);
  keys[code] = sources.size > 0;
  if (!sources.size) heldKeys.delete(code);
}

  root.id = 'mobile-controls';
