export let hudClock = 0;
const _txt = new WeakMap(), _html = new WeakMap(), _cls = new WeakMap(), _disp = new WeakMap(), _attr = new WeakMap();
function setText(el, v) { if (el && _txt.get(el) !== v) { _txt.set(el, v); el.textContent = v; } }
function setHTML(el, v) { if (el && _html.get(el) !== v) { _html.set(el, v); el.innerHTML = v; } }
function setClass(el, v) { if (el && _cls.get(el) !== v) { _cls.set(el, v); el.className = v; } }
function setDisp(el, v) { if (el && _disp.get(el) !== v) { _disp.set(el, v); el.style.display = v; } }
function setAttr(el, k, v) { const m = _attr.get(el) || {}; if (m[k] !== v) { m[k] = v; _attr.set(el, m); el.setAttribute(k, v); } }
function setWidth(el, v) { if (el && el.__w !== v) { el.__w = v; el.style.width = v; } }
let hotbarEls = null;
function buildHotbar() {
const hb = document.getElementById('hotbar-container');
hb.innerHTML = '';
hotbarEls = [];
for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
const div = document.createElement('div');
div.className = 'hotbar-slot w-14 h-14 p-1.5 flex flex-col justify-between cursor-pointer';
div.innerHTML = `
<div class="flex justify-between items-center text-[9px] font-tactical font-bold leading-none">
<span data-num class="text-slate-400">${i + 1}</span>
<span data-count class="text-slate-300"></span>
</div>
<div data-icon class="flex items-center justify-center my-auto"></div>
<div data-name class="text-[8px] font-tactical uppercase text-center truncate text-slate-200 leading-none"></div>
`;
div.onclick = () => { gameState.activeLeftSlot = i; syncEquippedMeshes(); updateHUD(true); };
hb.appendChild(div);
hotbarEls.push({ root: div, num: div.querySelector('[data-num]'), count: div.querySelector('[data-count]'), icon: div.querySelector('[data-icon]'), name: div.querySelector('[data-name]') });
}
}
function updateHotbar() {
if (!hotbarEls) buildHotbar();
for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
const slot = gameState.leftHotbar[i];
const item = slot ? LEFT_ITEMS[slot.id] : null;
const active = i === gameState.activeLeftSlot;
const el = hotbarEls[i];
el.root.classList.toggle('active', active);
setClass(el.num, active ? 'text-sky-300' : 'text-slate-400');
setText(el.count, slot && slot.count > 1 ? 'x' + slot.count : '');
setHTML(el.icon, item ? item.svg : '<span class="text-[10px]">-</span>');
const color = item ? item.color : '#475569';
if (el.icon.__c !== color) { el.icon.__c = color; el.icon.style.color = color; }
setText(el.name, item ? item.name.split(' ')[0] : 'EMPTY');
}
}
export function updateHUD(force) {
const hn = performance.now();
if (!force && hn - hudClock < 50) return;
hudClock = hn;
setText(document.getElementById('hud-cash'), Math.floor(gameState.cash).toLocaleString());
setText(document.getElementById('hud-zone'), gameState.zoneName);
setText(document.getElementById('hud-pov'), gameState.inCar
? (gameState.thirdPerson ? 'AERIAL 3RD' : 'DRIVER CAB')
: (gameState.thirdPerson ? '3RD PERSON' : '1ST PERSON'));
const clanBadge = document.getElementById('clan-alert-badge');
if (activeClanAlertText && !meteorDrop.active) {
setText(clanBadge, activeClanAlertText);
setClass(clanBadge, activeClanAlertText.startsWith('âš ')
? 'glass-panel px-3 py-1.5 text-[11px] font-tactical font-bold uppercase border-l-2 border-l-rose-500 text-rose-300 animate-pulse'
: 'glass-panel px-3 py-1.5 text-[11px] font-tactical font-bold uppercase border-l-2 border-l-amber-400 text-amber-300');
} else {
setClass(clanBadge, 'hidden');
}
const hpPct = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, (gameState.hp / gameState.maxHp) * 100));
const stPct = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, gameState.stamina));
const hpRing = document.getElementById('hud-hp-ring');
const stamRing = document.getElementById('hud-stam-ring');
if (hpRing) {
const off = (333.01 * (1 - hpPct / 100)).toFixed(2);
if (hpRing.__off !== off) { hpRing.__off = off; hpRing.style.strokeDashoffset = off; }
const col = hpPct > 50 ? '#10b981' : hpPct > 25 ? '#f59e0b' : '#ef4444';
if (hpRing.__col !== col) { hpRing.__col = col; hpRing.style.stroke = col; }
}
if (stamRing) {
const off = (257.61 * (1 - stPct / 100)).toFixed(2);
if (stamRing.__off !== off) { stamRing.__off = off; stamRing.style.strokeDashoffset = off; }
}
setText(document.getElementById('hud-hp-val'), String(Math.ceil(gameState.hp)));
setText(document.getElementById('hud-stam-val'), `${Math.round(stPct)}%`);
const rw = RIGHT_WEAPONS[gameState.activeRightWeapon] || RIGHT_WEAPONS.fists;
setHTML(document.getElementById('hud-rhand-icon'), rw.svg);
setText(document.getElementById('hud-rhand-name'), rw.name);
const lSlot = gameState.leftHotbar[gameState.activeLeftSlot];
const lItem = lSlot ? LEFT_ITEMS[lSlot.id] : null;
setHTML(document.getElementById('hud-lhand-icon'), lItem ? lItem.svg : '<span class="text-[10px] text-slate-500">&mdash;</span>');
setText(document.getElementById('hud-lhand-name'), lItem ? `${lItem.name}${lSlot.count > 1 ? ' x' + lSlot.count : ''}` : 'EMPTY');
const ammoCard = document.getElementById('ammo-hud-card');
if (gameState.activeRightWeapon === 'hotdog_shooter' && !gameState.inCar) {
setDisp(ammoCard, 'flex');
const countEl = document.getElementById('hud-ammo-count');
const hintEl = document.getElementById('hud-ammo-hint');
const barEl = document.getElementById('hud-sausage-bar');
setText(countEl, `${gameState.hotdogAmmo} / ${MAX_HOTDOG_AMMO}`);
setClass(countEl, gameState.hotdogAmmo === 0
? 'font-display text-sm text-rose-400 animate-pulse'
: gameState.hotdogAmmo <= 12 ? 'font-display text-sm text-amber-400' : 'font-display text-sm text-white');
setWidth(barEl, `${Math.round((gameState.hotdogAmmo / MAX_HOTDOG_AMMO) * 100)}%`);
setHTML(hintEl, gameState.hotdogAmmo === 0
? '<span class="text-rose-400 font-bold">EMPTY! REFILL AT HOTDOG CART/STORE</span>'
: '<span>[RMB] AIM</span><span class="text-amber-400">[LMB] FIRE</span>');
} else {
setDisp(ammoCard, 'none');
}
setDisp(document.getElementById('vehicle-hud'), gameState.inCar ? 'flex' : 'none');
setDisp(document.getElementById('onfoot-hotbar'), gameState.inCar ? 'none' : 'block');
const isScoped = gameState.isAiming && gameState.activeRightWeapon === 'hotdog_shooter' && !gameState.inCar;
setDisp(document.getElementById('sniper-scope'), isScoped ? 'flex' : 'none');
setDisp(document.getElementById('crosshair'), isScoped ? 'none' : 'flex');
if (gameState.inCar) {
const v = vehicles[gameState.activeCarIndex];
if (v) {
setText(document.getElementById('veh-name'), v.name);
const gLabel = GEAR_LABELS[v.gearIndex];
const gearCap = GEAR_CAPS_MPH[v.gearIndex];
setText(document.getElementById('veh-gear'), gLabel);
setText(document.getElementById('veh-gear-cap'), v.gearIndex === 1 ? 'NEUTRAL' : `MAX ${gearCap}`);
const mph = Math.round(Math.abs(v.speed) * 2.237);
setText(document.getElementById('veh-mph-text'), String(mph));
setText(document.getElementById('veh-rpm-text'), String(Math.round(v.rpm)));
const rpmDeg = -125 + Math.min(1, v.rpm / 8000) * 250;
const mphDeg = -125 + Math.min(1, mph / 200) * 250;
setAttr(document.getElementById('veh-rpm-needle'), 'transform', `rotate(${rpmDeg.toFixed(1)} 60 60)`);
setAttr(document.getElementById('veh-mph-needle'), 'transform', `rotate(${mphDeg.toFixed(1)} 60 60)`);
const normRpm = v.rpm / 8000;
const torqueEff = v.gearIndex === 1 ? 0 : Math.max(0.28, 1 - Math.pow((normRpm - 0.62) / 0.65, 2));
setWidth(document.getElementById('veh-torque-bar'), `${Math.round(torqueEff * 100)}%`);
const shiftLight = document.getElementById('veh-shift-light');
if (v.rpm >= 7150 && v.gearIndex >= 2 && v.gearIndex < 7) {
setText(shiftLight, 'SHIFT UP [I]');
setClass(shiftLight, 'px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[9px] font-tactical font-bold uppercase bg-rose-500 text-white animate-pulse');
} else if (v.gearIndex > 2 && mph < GEAR_CAPS_MPH[v.gearIndex - 1] * 0.58) {
setText(shiftLight, 'DOWNSHIFT [K]');
setClass(shiftLight, 'px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[9px] font-tactical font-bold uppercase bg-amber-500/30 text-amber-300');
} else {
setText(shiftLight, v.isSupercar ? 'SUPERCHARGED' : 'MANUAL');
setClass(shiftLight, v.isSupercar
? 'px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[9px] font-tactical font-bold uppercase bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300'
: 'px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[9px] font-tactical font-bold uppercase bg-slate-800 text-slate-300');
}
}
}
updateHotbar();
}

export function showWeaponWheel() {
  const wheel = document.getElementById('radial-menu');
  const nodes = document.getElementById('wheel-nodes');
  const activeW = RIGHT_WEAPONS[gameState.activeRightWeapon] || RIGHT_WEAPONS.fists;
  document.getElementById('wheel-center-name').textContent = activeW.name;
  document.getElementById('wheel-center-dmg').textContent = activeW.id === 'hotdog_shooter'
    ? `AMMO ${gameState.hotdogAmmo}/${MAX_HOTDOG_AMMO}`
    : `DMG ${activeW.damage}`;

  nodes.innerHTML = '';
  const list = gameState.rightWeapons;
  list.forEach((wId, idx) => {
    const w = RIGHT_WEAPONS[wId];
    if (!w) return;
    const ang = -Math.PI / 2 + (idx / list.length) * Math.PI * 2;
    const r = 86;
    const cx = 144 + Math.cos(ang) * r;
    const cy = 144 + Math.sin(ang) * r;
    const isAct = wId === gameState.activeRightWeapon;
    const node = document.createElement('div');
    node.className = `absolute -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-12 h-12 rounded-full flex flex-col items-center justify-center border ${isAct ? 'bg-amber-500 text-slate-950 border-amber-200 scale-110' : 'bg-slate-900/95 text-slate-200 border-white/20'}`;
    node.style.left = `${cx}px`;
    node.style.top = `${cy}px`;
    node.innerHTML = `${w.svg}<span class="text-[7px] font-tactical font-bold uppercase mt-0.5">${w.name.split(' ')[0]}</span>`;
    nodes.appendChild(node);
  });

  wheel.style.display = 'flex';
  gameState.wheelTimer = 1.35;
}

export function cycleRightWeapon(dir = 1) {
  if (gameState.inCar || meteorDrop.active) return;
  const list = gameState.rightWeapons;
  const idx = list.indexOf(gameState.activeRightWeapon);
  const nextIdx = (idx + dir + list.length) % list.length;
  gameState.activeRightWeapon = list[nextIdx];
  if (gameState.activeRightWeapon !== 'hotdog_shooter') {
    gameState.isAiming = false;
  }
  sfx.uiClick();
  syncEquippedMeshes();
  updateHUD();
  showWeaponWheel();
}

const mapStaticCache = new Map();
const MAP_WORLD = { minX: -720, maxX: 560, minZ: -600, maxZ: 340 };
function buildStaticMap(scale) {
const w = Math.ceil((MAP_WORLD.maxX - MAP_WORLD.minX) * scale);
const h = Math.ceil((MAP_WORLD.maxZ - MAP_WORLD.minZ) * scale);
const cv = document.createElement('canvas');
cv.width = w; cv.height = h;
const ctx = cv.getContext('2d');
const X = wx => (wx - MAP_WORLD.minX) * scale;
const Y = wz => (wz - MAP_WORLD.minZ) * scale;
ctx.fillStyle = '#365e33'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
mapShapes.forEach(s => { if (s.w > 200 && s.color === '#d97706') { ctx.fillStyle = s.color; ctx.fillRect(X(s.x - s.w / 2), Y(s.z - s.d / 2), s.w * scale, s.d * scale); } });
ctx.fillStyle = '#1f2937';
[-120, -72, -24, 24, 72, 120].forEach(rx => ctx.fillRect(X(rx - 6), Y(-174), 12 * scale, 252 * scale));
[-168, -120, -72, -24, 24, 72].forEach(rz => ctx.fillRect(X(-126), Y(rz - 6), 252 * scale, 12 * scale));
mapShapes.forEach(s => {
if (s.w > 200 && s.color === '#d97706') return;
ctx.fillStyle = s.color;
ctx.fillRect(X(s.x - s.w / 2), Y(s.z - s.d / 2), s.w * scale, s.d * scale);
if (s.border) { ctx.strokeStyle = s.border; ctx.lineWidth = s.enterable ? 1.5 : 1; ctx.strokeRect(X(s.x - s.w / 2), Y(s.z - s.d / 2), s.w * scale, s.d * scale); }
});
sausageRefillZones.forEach(rz => { ctx.fillStyle = '#facc15'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(X(rz.x), Y(rz.z), 3.2, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill(); });
ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(120,130,120,.55)'; ctx.fillRect(X(-138), Y(-590), 508 * scale, 420 * scale);
ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(139,125,86,.6)'; ctx.fillRect(X(-560), Y(-172), 364 * scale, 344 * scale);
ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(79,138,63,.85)'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(X(-480), Y(-45), 70 * scale, 0, 6.3); ctx.fill();
ctx.strokeStyle = '#e9c33a'; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.beginPath();
for (let q = 0; q <= 274; q += 6) { const x = -196 - q; q ? ctx.lineTo(X(x), Y(ahRz(x))) : ctx.moveTo(X(x), Y(ahRz(x))); }
ctx.stroke();
ctx.strokeStyle = '#38bdf8'; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.beginPath();
for (let z = -176; z > -584; z -= 8) { z === -176 ? ctx.moveTo(X(riverX(z)), Y(z)) : ctx.lineTo(X(riverX(z)), Y(z)); }
ctx.stroke();
ctx.strokeStyle = '#e9c33a'; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.beginPath();
for (let z = -170; z > -584; z -= 8) { const px = X(roadC(z)), py = Y(z); z === -170 ? ctx.moveTo(px, py) : ctx.lineTo(px, py); }
ctx.stroke();
if (aura.hospitalSpawn) {
const hx = aura.hospitalSpawn.x + 14, hz = aura.hospitalSpawn.z + 7.4;
ctx.fillStyle = '#fff'; ctx.fillRect(X(hx) - 5, Y(hz) - 5, 10, 10);
ctx.fillStyle = '#dc2626'; ctx.fillRect(X(hx) - 4, Y(hz) - 1, 8, 2); ctx.fillRect(X(hx) - 1, Y(hz) - 4, 2, 8);
}
ctx.fillStyle = '#fff'; ctx.font = 'bold 10px sans-serif';
ctx.fillText('AURA HILLS', X(-470), Y(-150)); ctx.fillText('MOUNTAINS', X(60), Y(-400));
return cv;
}
export function renderMapCanvasBase(canvasEl, centerOnPlayer) {
  const ctx = canvasEl.getContext('2d');
  const W = canvasEl.width;
  const H = canvasEl.height;
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);

  ctx.fillStyle = '#365e33';
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);

  const scale = centerOnPlayer ? 0.65 : 0.38;
  const cx = W / 2;
  const cy = H / 2;
  const refX = centerOnPlayer ? player.x : -29;
  const refZ = centerOnPlayer ? player.z : -190;

  const toCanvasX = (wx) => cx + (wx - refX) * scale;
  const toCanvasY = (wz) => cy + (wz - refZ) * scale;

  mapShapes.forEach((s) => {
    if (s.w > 200 && s.color === '#d97706') {
      ctx.fillStyle = s.color;
      ctx.fillRect(toCanvasX(s.x - s.w / 2), toCanvasY(s.z - s.d / 2), s.w * scale, s.d * scale);
    }
  });

  ctx.fillStyle = '#1f2937';
  [-120, -72, -24, 24, 72, 120].forEach((rx) => {
    ctx.fillRect(toCanvasX(rx - 6), toCanvasY(-174), 12 * scale, 252 * scale);
  });
  [-168, -120, -72, -24, 24, 72].forEach((rz) => {
    ctx.fillRect(toCanvasX(-126), toCanvasY(rz - 6), 252 * scale, 12 * scale);
  });

  mapShapes.forEach((s) => {
    if (s.w > 200 && s.color === '#d97706') return;
    ctx.fillStyle = s.color;
    ctx.fillRect(toCanvasX(s.x - s.w / 2), toCanvasY(s.z - s.d / 2), s.w * scale, s.d * scale);
    if (s.border) {
      ctx.strokeStyle = s.border;
      ctx.lineWidth = s.enterable ? 1.5 : 1;
      ctx.strokeRect(toCanvasX(s.x - s.w / 2), toCanvasY(s.z - s.d / 2), s.w * scale, s.d * scale);
    }
  });

  vehicles.forEach((v) => {
    ctx.fillStyle = v.isSupercar ? '#f59e0b' : '#f43f5e';
    ctx.fillRect(toCanvasX(v.x) - 3.5, toCanvasY(v.z) - 3.5, 7, 7);
  });

  sausageRefillZones.forEach((rz) => {
    ctx.fillStyle = '#facc15';
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(toCanvasX(rz.x), toCanvasY(rz.z), 3.2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();
  });

  npcs.forEach((npc) => {
    if (npc.aggro === 'clan' && !npc.asleep) {
      const clan = CLAN_DEFS[npc.clanId];
      ctx.fillStyle = clan ? clan.hex : '#ef4444';
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(toCanvasX(npc.x), toCanvasY(npc.z), 2.8, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
    }
  });

  if (centerOnPlayer) {
    const px = cx;
    const py = cy;
    const fwdX = -Math.sin(player.yaw);
    const fwdY = -Math.cos(player.yaw);
    ctx.save();
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(px, py);
    ctx.arc(px, py, 24, Math.atan2(fwdY, fwdX) - 0.5, Math.atan2(fwdY, fwdX) + 0.5);
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.25)';
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(px, py, 4.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#38bdf8';
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffffff';
    ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.restore();

    const northEl = document.getElementById('minimap-north');
    if (northEl) {
      northEl.style.left = '89px';
      northEl.style.top = '9px';
    }
  } else {
    const px = toCanvasX(player.x);
    const py = toCanvasY(player.z);
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(px, py, 5.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#38bdf8';
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffffff';
    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    ctx.stroke();
  }
}

export function setScreen(nextScreen) {
  gameState.screen = nextScreen;
  document.body.classList.toggle('menu-active', nextScreen === 'start');
  hooks.setAircraftScreen(nextScreen);
  hooks.syncMobileControls(true);
  document.getElementById('start-menu').style.display = nextScreen === 'start' ? 'flex' : 'none';
  document.getElementById('hud').style.display = nextScreen === 'playing' ? 'block' : 'none';
  document.getElementById('pause-menu').style.display = nextScreen === 'paused' ? 'flex' : 'none';

  if (nextScreen !== 'playing') {
    inputState.isPrimaryFireHeld = false;
    gameState.isAiming = false;
    closeSubModals();
    sfx.updateEngine(false, 0, false);
    if (document.pointerLockElement === canvas) {
      document.exitPointerLock();
    }
  } else {
    updateHUD();
  }
}

export function closeSubModals() {
  gameState.inventoryOpen = false;
  gameState.mapOpen = false;
  gameState.settingsOpen = false;
  document.getElementById('inventory-menu').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('map-modal').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('settings-menu').style.display = 'none';
}

export function openInventoryModal() {
  if (gameState.screen !== 'playing' || gameState.inCar || meteorDrop.active) return;
  closeSubModals();
  gameState.inventoryOpen = true;
  gameState.inspectedLeftSlot = gameState.activeLeftSlot;
  if (document.pointerLockElement === canvas) document.exitPointerLock();
  renderInventoryModal();
  document.getElementById('inventory-menu').style.display = 'flex';
  hooks.syncMobileControls(true);
}

export function closeInventoryModal() {
  gameState.inventoryOpen = false;
  document.getElementById('inventory-menu').style.display = 'none';
  hooks.syncMobileControls(true);
}

export function renderInventoryModal() {
  const grid = document.getElementById('inv-left-grid');
  grid.innerHTML = '';
  for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
    const slot = gameState.leftHotbar[i];
    const item = slot ? LEFT_ITEMS[slot.id] : null;
    const selected = i === gameState.inspectedLeftSlot;
    const equipped = i === gameState.activeLeftSlot;
    const btn = document.createElement('button');
    btn.className = `p-2 rounded-lg border text-center flex flex-col items-center justify-between h-20 cursor-pointer ${selected ? 'border-sky-400 bg-sky-950/60' : 'border-white/15 bg-slate-900/80'}`;
    btn.innerHTML = `
      <div class="w-full flex justify-between text-[9px] font-tactical">
        <span class="text-slate-400">${i + 1}</span>
        <span class="text-sky-300 font-bold">${equipped ? 'EQ' : ''}</span>
      </div>
      <div style="color:${item ? item.color : '#475569'}">${item ? item.svg : '-'}</div>
      <div class="text-[9px] font-tactical uppercase text-slate-200 truncate w-full">${item ? item.name.split(' ')[0] : 'EMPTY'}</div>
    `;
    btn.onclick = () => {
      gameState.inspectedLeftSlot = i;
      gameState.activeLeftSlot = i;
      syncEquippedMeshes();
      updateHUD();
      renderInventoryModal();
    };
    grid.appendChild(btn);
  }

  const inspSlot = gameState.leftHotbar[gameState.inspectedLeftSlot];
  const inspItem = inspSlot ? LEFT_ITEMS[inspSlot.id] : null;
  const inspBox = document.getElementById('inv-left-inspector');
  if (inspItem) {
    inspBox.innerHTML = `
      <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
        <span class="font-display text-lg uppercase text-white">${inspItem.name} <span class="text-xs text-slate-400">(x${inspSlot.count})</span></span>
        <span class="text-xs font-tactical font-bold text-sky-400">${inspItem.stat}</span>
      </div>
      <p class="text-xs text-slate-300 mt-1">${inspItem.desc}</p>
    `;
  } else {
    inspBox.innerHTML = `
      <div class="font-display text-base uppercase text-slate-500">EMPTY UTILITY SLOT</div>
      <p class="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1">Smash crates or explore walk-in buildings to collect Medkits, Energy Drinks, Burgers, and Cash.</p>
    `;
  }

  const rList = document.getElementById('inv-right-list');
  rList.innerHTML = '';
  gameState.rightWeapons.forEach((wId) => {
    const w = RIGHT_WEAPONS[wId];
    if (!w) return;
    const isEq = wId === gameState.activeRightWeapon;
    const row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = `p-3 rounded-lg border flex items-center justify-between cursor-pointer ${isEq ? 'border-amber-400 bg-amber-500/15' : 'border-white/10 bg-slate-900/75 hover:border-white/30'}`;
    const statBadge = w.id === 'hotdog_shooter'
      ? `AMMO ${gameState.hotdogAmmo}/${MAX_HOTDOG_AMMO}`
      : `DMG ${w.damage}`;
    row.innerHTML = `
      <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
        <div class="w-8 h-8 rounded bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-400 flex items-center justify-center">${w.svg}</div>
        <div>
          <div class="font-display text-sm uppercase text-white">${w.name}</div>
          <div class="text-[11px] text-slate-400">${w.desc}</div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="text-right shrink-0 ml-2">
        <div class="text-xs font-tactical font-bold text-amber-400">${statBadge}</div>
        <div class="text-[10px] font-tactical uppercase ${isEq ? 'text-emerald-400' : 'text-slate-400'}">${isEq ? 'EQUIPPED' : 'CLICK TO EQUIP'}</div>
      </div>
    `;
    row.onclick = () => {
      gameState.activeRightWeapon = wId;
      if (wId !== 'hotdog_shooter') gameState.isAiming = false;
      sfx.uiClick();
      syncEquippedMeshes();
      updateHUD();
      renderInventoryModal();
    };
    rList.appendChild(row);
  });
}

export function openSettingsModal() {
  gameState.settingsOpen = true;
  hooks.syncMobileControls(true);
  const sensSlider = document.getElementById('setting-sens');
  sensSlider.value = gameState.settings.sensitivity;
  document.getElementById('setting-sens-val').textContent = `${gameState.settings.sensitivity.toFixed(2)}x`;
  syncSettingsButtons();
  document.getElementById('settings-menu').style.display = 'flex';
}

export function closeSettingsModal() {
  gameState.settingsOpen = false;
  document.getElementById('settings-menu').style.display = 'none';
  hooks.syncMobileControls(true);
}

export function syncSettingsButtons() {
  const tier = gameState.settings.graphics;
  document.querySelectorAll('.gfx-choice').forEach((btn) => {
    const isMatch = btn.dataset.gfx === tier;
    btn.className = isMatch
      ? 'gfx-choice menu-btn-primary py-2.5 px-3 text-center font-display text-sm uppercase cursor-pointer'
      : 'gfx-choice menu-btn py-2.5 px-3 text-center font-display text-sm uppercase text-slate-200 cursor-pointer';
  });
  const descMap = {
    low: ['LOW â€¢ HIGH FPS', 'Flat low-poly shading without procedural textures or dynamic shadows for maximum frame rate.'],
    medium: ['MEDIUM â€¢ PBR TEXTURES', 'Procedural PBR textures with metallic clearcoat vehicles, real glass windows, proximity LODs, and 1024x1024 shadows.'],
    high: ['HIGH â€¢ ULTRA PBR & SKY', 'Full procedural bump maps, 2048x2048 soft shadows, architectural trim, and atmospheric sky dome.']
  };
  document.getElementById('setting-gfx-desc').textContent = descMap[tier][0];
  document.getElementById('setting-gfx-detail').textContent = descMap[tier][1];

  const sfxBtn = document.getElementById('btn-setting-sfx');
  sfxBtn.textContent = gameState.settings.sfx ? 'ENABLED' : 'MUTED';
  sfxBtn.className = gameState.settings.sfx
    ? 'menu-btn px-4 py-2 font-display text-xs uppercase text-emerald-400 border-emerald-500/40 cursor-pointer'
    : 'menu-btn px-4 py-2 font-display text-xs uppercase text-rose-400 border-rose-500/40 cursor-pointer';

  const mobileBtn = document.getElementById('btn-setting-mobile');
  if (mobileBtn) {
    mobileBtn.textContent = gameState.settings.mobileControls ? 'ENABLED' : 'DISABLED';
    mobileBtn.className = gameState.settings.mobileControls
      ? 'menu-btn px-4 py-2 font-display text-xs uppercase text-emerald-400 border-emerald-500/40 cursor-pointer'
      : 'menu-btn px-4 py-2 font-display text-xs uppercase text-rose-400 border-rose-500/40 cursor-pointer';
  }
}

export function openMapModal() {
  if (gameState.screen !== 'playing' || meteorDrop.active) return;
  closeSubModals();
  hooks.syncMobileControls(true);
  gameState.mapOpen = true;
  if (document.pointerLockElement === canvas) document.exitPointerLock();
  renderMapCanvas(document.getElementById('fullmap-canvas'), false);
  document.getElementById('map-modal').style.display = 'flex';
}

export function closeMapModal() {
  gameState.mapOpen = false;
  document.getElementById('map-modal').style.display = 'none';
  hooks.syncMobileControls(true);
}

export function exportSaveFile() {
  const payload = {
    title: 'Grand Theft Aura',
    version: 7,
    timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
    player: { x: player.x, y: player.y, z: player.z, yaw: player.yaw, pitch: player.pitch },
    state: {
      hp: gameState.hp,
      stamina: gameState.stamina,
      cash: gameState.cash,
      hotdogAmmo: gameState.hotdogAmmo,
      rightWeapons: gameState.rightWeapons,
      activeRightWeapon: gameState.activeRightWeapon,
      leftHotbar: gameState.leftHotbar,
      activeLeftSlot: gameState.activeLeftSlot,
      thirdPerson: gameState.thirdPerson,
      settings: gameState.settings
    }
  };
  const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2)], { type: 'application/json' });
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = url;
  a.download = `grand-theft-aura-save-${Date.now()}.json`;
  document.body.appendChild(a);
  a.click();
  a.remove();
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  sfx.pickup();
  pushFeed('GAME SAVED (.JSON EXPORTED)', 'border-l-emerald-400');
}

export function importSaveFromFile(file) {
  if (!file) return;
  const reader = new FileReader();
  reader.onload = (ev) => {
    try {
      const data = JSON.parse(ev.target.result);
      if (!data || !data.player || !data.state) throw new Error('Invalid Grand Theft Aura save file.');
      if (gameState.inCar) exitCurrentCar(true);
      meteorDrop.active = false;
      meteorGroup.visible = false;
      meteorReticle.visible = false;
      document.getElementById('meteor-aim-banner').style.display = 'none';

      player.x = Number(data.player.x) || -11.5;
      player.y = Math.max(0.18, Number(data.player.y) || 0.18);
      player.z = Number(data.player.z) || 18.5;
      player.yaw = Number(data.player.yaw) || 0;
      player.pitch = Number(data.player.pitch) || 0;
      player.vy = 0;
      player.grounded = true;
      player.peakAirY = player.y;

      const st = data.state;
      gameState.hp = Math.min(gameState.maxHp, Math.max(10, Number(st.hp) || gameState.maxHp));
      gameState.stamina = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, Number(st.stamina) || 100));
      gameState.cash = Math.max(0, Number(st.cash) || 0);
      gameState.hotdogAmmo = st.hotdogAmmo !== undefined ? Math.max(0, Math.min(MAX_HOTDOG_AMMO, Number(st.hotdogAmmo))) : MAX_HOTDOG_AMMO;
      gameState.rightWeapons = Array.isArray(st.rightWeapons) && st.rightWeapons.length ? st.rightWeapons : ['fists', 'bat'];
      gameState.activeRightWeapon = RIGHT_WEAPONS[st.activeRightWeapon] ? st.activeRightWeapon : 'fists';
      gameState.leftHotbar = Array.isArray(st.leftHotbar) && st.leftHotbar.length === 5 ? st.leftHotbar : gameState.leftHotbar;
      gameState.activeLeftSlot = Math.min(4, Math.max(0, Number(st.activeLeftSlot) || 0));
      gameState.thirdPerson = Boolean(st.thirdPerson);

      if (st.settings) {
        gameState.settings.sensitivity = Number(st.settings.sensitivity) || 1.0;
        gameState.settings.graphics = ['low', 'medium', 'high'].includes(st.settings.graphics) ? st.settings.graphics : 'medium';
        gameState.settings.sfx = st.settings.sfx !== false;
        sfx.enabled = gameState.settings.sfx;
        applyGraphicsTier(gameState.settings.graphics);
      }

      syncEquippedMeshes();
      setScreen('playing');
      pushFeed('SAVE FILE LOADED SUCCESSFULLY', 'border-l-sky-400');
    } catch (err) {
      const banner = document.getElementById('error-banner');
      banner.textContent = `Save Import Error: ${err.message}`;
      banner.style.display = 'block';
      setTimeout(() => { banner.style.display = 'none'; }, 3500);
    }
  };
  reader.readAsText(file);
}

export function buildAuraUI(){
  const st=document.createElement('style');st.textContent=`
  .glass-panel{background:linear-gradient(160deg,rgba(18,24,34,.88),rgba(8,11,17,.9))!important;border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.1)!important;border-top:1px solid rgba(245,158,11,.5)!important;box-shadow:0 12px 32px rgba(0,0,0,.6),inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,.06)!important}
  #ao-stars{position:fixed;top:14px;left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%);display:none;gap:4px;font-size:30px;padding:4px 16px;border-radius:999px;background:rgba(8,11,17,.7);border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.15);text-shadow:0 0 12px currentColor;z-index:25}
  #ao-compass{position:fixed;top:14px;right:208px;padding:6px 14px;font:700 12px Rajdhani,sans-serif;letter-spacing:.15em;color:#e2e8f0;border-radius:8px;background:rgba(8,11,17,.72);border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.12);z-index:25}
  #ao-vig{position:fixed;inset:0;pointer-events:none;z-index:5;background:radial-gradient(ellipse at center,transparent 58%,rgba(0,0,0,.42) 100%)}`;
  document.head.appendChild(st);
  const hud=document.getElementById('hud');
  const s=document.createElement('div');s.id='ao-stars';hud.appendChild(s);
  const c=document.createElement('div');c.id='ao-compass';hud.appendChild(c);
  const v=document.createElement('div');v.id='ao-vig';document.body.appendChild(v);
  const tip=document.createElement('div');tip.id='ao-tip';tip.style.cssText='position:fixed;bottom:150px;left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%);padding:6px 14px;border-radius:8px;background:rgba(8,11,17,.85);border:1px solid #f59e0b;font:700 13px Rajdhani,sans-serif;letter-spacing:.1em;display:none;z-index:25';hud.appendChild(tip);
}

export function renderMapCanvas(cv,center){renderMapCanvasBase(cv,center);
  const ctx=cv.getContext('2d'),sc=center?.65:.38,rx=center?player.x:-29,rz=center?player.z:-190,X=w=>cv.width/2+(w-rx)*sc,Y=w=>cv.height/2+(w-rz)*sc;
  ctx.fillStyle='rgba(120,130,120,.55)';ctx.fillRect(X(-138),Y(-170)-420*sc,508*sc,420*sc);
  ctx.fillStyle='rgba(139,125,86,.6)';ctx.fillRect(X(-560),Y(-172),364*sc,344*sc);
  ctx.fillStyle='rgba(79,138,63,.85)';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(X(-480),Y(-45),70*sc,0,6.3);ctx.fill();
  ctx.strokeStyle='#e9c33a';ctx.lineWidth=2;ctx.beginPath();for(let q=0;q<=274;q+=6){const x=-196-q;q?ctx.lineTo(X(x),Y(ahRz(x))):ctx.moveTo(X(x),Y(ahRz(x)));}ctx.stroke();
  ctx.strokeStyle='#38bdf8';ctx.lineWidth=2;ctx.beginPath();for(let z=-176;z>-584;z-=8){z===-176?ctx.moveTo(X(riverX(z)),Y(z)):ctx.lineTo(X(riverX(z)),Y(z));}ctx.stroke();
  if(aura.hospitalSpawn){const hx=aura.hospitalSpawn.x+14,hz=aura.hospitalSpawn.z+7.4;ctx.fillStyle='#fff';ctx.fillRect(X(hx)-5,Y(hz)-5,10,10);ctx.fillStyle='#dc2626';ctx.fillRect(X(hx)-4,Y(hz)-1,8,2);ctx.fillRect(X(hx)-1,Y(hz)-4,2,8);}
  ctx.fillStyle='#fff';ctx.font='bold 10px sans-serif';ctx.fillText('AURA HILLS',X(-470),Y(-150));ctx.fillText('MOUNTAINS',X(60),Y(-400));
  ctx.strokeStyle='#e9c33a';ctx.lineWidth=2;ctx.beginPath();for(let z=-170;z>-584;z-=8){const px=X(roadC(z)),py=Y(z);z===-170?ctx.moveTo(px,py):ctx.lineTo(px,py);}ctx.stroke();
  const f=(x,z,col,r)=>{ctx.fillStyle=col;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(X(x),Y(z),r,0,6.3);ctx.fill();};
  if(!under()){AO.cops.forEach(c=>f(c.x,c.z,'#3b82f6',3));AO.cars.forEach(c=>f(c.x,c.z,Math.sin(performance.now()/120)>0?'#ef4444':'#3b82f6',4.5));manholes.forEach(m=>f(m[0],m[1],'#34d399',2));}
  hooks.drawMapOverlay(ctx,cv,center);
  hooks.drawAircraftMapOverlay(cv,center);
}
/** Attach all DOM / window listeners. Call once from main.js (never from Studio). */
export function initUI() {
buildAuraUI();

document.getElementById('btn-start-play').onclick = () => {
  sfx.unlock();
  sfx.uiClick();
  setScreen('playing');
  triggerMeteorLanding();
  if (canvas.requestPointerLock) {
    try {
      if (!gameState.settings.mobileControls || navigator.maxTouchPoints === 0) {
        const p = canvas.requestPointerLock();
        if (p && p.catch) p.catch(() => {});
      }
    } catch (_) {}
  }
};

document.getElementById('btn-start-settings').onclick = () => {
  sfx.unlock();
  sfx.uiClick();
  openSettingsModal();
};

document.getElementById('btn-start-import').onclick = () => {
  sfx.unlock();
  sfx.uiClick();
  document.getElementById('save-file-input').click();
};

document.getElementById('btn-pause-resume').onclick = () => { sfx.uiClick(); setScreen('playing'); };

document.getElementById('btn-pause-settings').onclick = () => { sfx.uiClick(); openSettingsModal(); };

document.getElementById('btn-pause-save').onclick = () => { sfx.uiClick(); exportSaveFile(); };

document.getElementById('btn-pause-import').onclick = () => {
  sfx.uiClick();
  document.getElementById('save-file-input').click();
};

document.getElementById('btn-pause-exit').onclick = () => {
  sfx.uiClick();
  if (gameState.inCar) exitCurrentCar(true);
  meteorDrop.active = false;
  meteorGroup.visible = false;
  meteorReticle.visible = false;
  document.getElementById('meteor-aim-banner').style.display = 'none';
  setScreen('start');
};

document.getElementById('btn-settings-close').onclick = () => { sfx.uiClick(); closeSettingsModal(); };

document.getElementById('setting-sens').oninput = (e) => {
  gameState.settings.sensitivity = parseFloat(e.target.value);
  document.getElementById('setting-sens-val').textContent = `${gameState.settings.sensitivity.toFixed(2)}x`;
};

document.querySelectorAll('.gfx-choice').forEach((btn) => {
  btn.onclick = () => {
    sfx.uiClick();
    applyGraphicsTier(btn.dataset.gfx);
    syncSettingsButtons();
  };
});

  document.getElementById('btn-setting-sfx').onclick = () => {
  gameState.settings.sfx = !gameState.settings.sfx;
  sfx.enabled = gameState.settings.sfx;
  sfx.uiClick();
  syncSettingsButtons();
};

document.getElementById('btn-setting-mobile').onclick = () => {
  gameState.settings.mobileControls = !gameState.settings.mobileControls;
  sfx.uiClick();
  syncSettingsButtons();
  hooks.persistMobilePreference();
};

document.getElementById('btn-inv-close').onclick = () => { sfx.uiClick(); closeInventoryModal(); };

document.getElementById('btn-inv-use').onclick = () => useLeftHandItem(gameState.inspectedLeftSlot);

document.getElementById('btn-inv-drop').onclick = () => dropLeftSlot(gameState.inspectedLeftSlot);

document.getElementById('btn-map-close').onclick = () => { sfx.uiClick(); closeMapModal(); };

document.getElementById('minimap-box').onclick = () => openMapModal();

document.getElementById('save-file-input').onchange = (e) => {
  if (e.target.files && e.target.files[0]) {
    importSaveFromFile(e.target.files[0]);
    e.target.value = '';
  }
};
}

// Register late-bound implementations for lower layers (see state.js).

// =============================================================================
// vehicles.js â€” Vehicle mesh builders, 6-speed manual gearbox, driving physics, suspension and exhaust flame effects.
// =============================================================================
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { levelData } from './levelData.js';
import { CAR_PTS, collidesHorizontally, computeSupportFloor, gameState, hooks, inputState, isInWater, keys, lampPosts, npcs, player, pushFeed, surfaceGrip, terrainH } from './state.js';
import { camera, makeMat, worldRoot } from './engine.js';
import { sfx } from './audio.js';
import { spawnParticles } from './items.js';

export const vehicles = [];

export const GEAR_LABELS = ['R', 'N', '1', '2', '3', '4', '5', '6'];

export const GEAR_CAPS_MPH = [36, 0, 39, 66, 95, 125, 154, 180];

export const GEAR_RATIO_MULT = [-0.55, 0, 1.0, 0.66, 0.46, 0.34, 0.26, 0.21];

export function buildSportsCarMesh({ name = 'SPORTS CAR', bodyHex = 0xdc2626, accentHex = 0xf8fafc, x = 0, z = 0, yaw = 0, isCoupe = true, isSupercar = false } = {}) {
  const root = new THREE.Group();
  root.userData.studioName = name;
  const carScale = 1.35;
  root.scale.setScalar(carScale);
  root.position.set(x, 0.12, z);
  root.rotation.y = yaw;

  const chassis = new THREE.Group();
  chassis.userData.studioName = 'Chassis';
  root.add(chassis);

  const bodyMat = makeMat(bodyHex, {
    isCarMetal: true,
    texType: 'metallicflake',
    repeatX: 6,
    repeatY: 6,
    metalness: isSupercar ? 0.90 : 0.86,
    roughness: isSupercar ? 0.12 : 0.16,
    clearcoat: 1.0,
    clearcoatRoughness: 0.05,
    envMapIntensity: 2.2,
    flatShading: false
  });
  const accentMat = makeMat(accentHex, {
    isCarMetal: true,
    metalness: 0.88,
    roughness: 0.18,
    clearcoat: 0.95,
    clearcoatRoughness: 0.06,
    flatShading: false
  });
  const chromeMat = makeMat(0xf8fafc, {
    texType: 'brushedsteel',
    metalness: 0.96,
    roughness: 0.10,
    envMapIntensity: 2.4,
    flatShading: false
  });
  const carbonMat = makeMat(0x0f172a, { roughness: 0.32, metalness: 0.55, clearcoat: 0.7, flatShading: false });
  const glassMat = makeMat(0x38bdf8, {
    metalness: 0.35,
    roughness: 0.12,
    clearcoat: 0.8,
    envMapIntensity: 1.2,
    transparent: true,
    opacity: 0.16,
    depthWrite: false,
    side: THREE.DoubleSide,
    flatShading: false
  });

  const lowerH = isSupercar ? 0.42 : 0.54;
  const lower = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.04, lowerH, 4.46), bodyMat);
  lower.userData.studioName = 'Body';
  lower.position.y = isSupercar ? 0.54 : 0.62;
  lower.castShadow = true;
  lower.receiveShadow = true;

  const sideSkirtL = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.12, 0.14, 2.7), carbonMat);
  sideSkirtL.position.set(-1.04, 0.34, 0);
  const sideSkirtR = sideSkirtL.clone();
  sideSkirtR.position.x = 1.04;

  const stripe = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.46, 0.02, 4.42), accentMat);
  stripe.position.y = lower.position.y + lowerH / 2 + 0.01;

  const cabinLen = isSupercar ? 1.85 : (isCoupe ? 2.0 : 2.65);
  const cabinH = isSupercar ? 0.56 : 0.78;
  const cabinZ = isSupercar ? -0.10 : (isCoupe ? -0.22 : -0.05);
  const cabin = new THREE.Group();
  cabin.userData.studioName = 'Cabin';
  cabin.position.set(0, lower.position.y + lowerH / 2 + cabinH / 2 - 0.02, cabinZ);
  const roof = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.74, 0.09, cabinLen), bodyMat);
  roof.userData.studioName = 'Cabin roof';
  roof.position.y = cabinH / 2 - 0.045;
  roof.castShadow = true;
  cabin.add(roof);

  const pillarGeo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.11, cabinH - 0.08, 0.11);
  for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
    for (const end of [-1, 1]) {
      const pillar = new THREE.Mesh(pillarGeo, bodyMat);
      pillar.position.set(side * 0.82, -0.02, end * (cabinLen / 2 - 0.06));
      pillar.castShadow = true;
      cabin.add(pillar);
    }
    const sill = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.12, 0.12, cabinLen - 0.12), bodyMat);
    sill.position.set(side * 0.82, -cabinH / 2 + 0.05, 0);
    cabin.add(sill);
  }

  const glassFront = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(1.48, cabinH * 0.74), glassMat);
  glassFront.position.set(0, -0.015, cabinLen / 2 - 0.035);
  glassFront.rotation.x = -0.16;
  const glassRear = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(1.48, cabinH * 0.68), glassMat);
  glassRear.position.set(0, -0.015, -cabinLen / 2 + 0.035);
  glassRear.rotation.y = Math.PI;
  const glassLeft = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(cabinLen * 0.76, cabinH * 0.64), glassMat);
  glassLeft.position.set(-0.79, -0.015, 0);
  glassLeft.rotation.y = -Math.PI / 2;
  const glassRight = glassLeft.clone();
  glassRight.position.x = 0.79;
  glassRight.rotation.y = Math.PI / 2;
  cabin.add(glassFront, glassRear, glassLeft, glassRight);

  const dashboard = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.62, 0.15, 0.42), carbonMat);
  dashboard.userData.studioName = 'Dashboard';
  dashboard.position.set(0, lower.position.y + lowerH / 2 + 0.08, 0.76);
  const seatBackHeight = isSupercar ? 0.25 : 0.38;
  const seats = [];
  for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
    const seat = new THREE.Group();
    const cushion = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.48, 0.12, 0.52), carbonMat);
    cushion.position.set(0, 0.83, -0.26);
    const back = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.48, seatBackHeight, 0.12), carbonMat);
    back.position.set(0, 0.88 + seatBackHeight / 2, -0.5);
    seat.add(cushion, back);
    seat.position.x = side * 0.4;
    seats.push(seat);
  }
  const steeringPivot = new THREE.Group();
  steeringPivot.userData.studioName = 'Steering assembly';
  steeringPivot.position.set(-0.42, cabin.position.y - (isSupercar ? 0.12 : 0.16), 0.54);
  const steeringWheel = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(0.25, 0.035, 8, 20), carbonMat);
  steeringWheel.userData.studioName = 'Steering wheel';
  steeringPivot.add(steeringWheel);
  const steeringHub = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.055, 0.055, 0.09, 8), chromeMat);
  steeringHub.userData.studioName = 'Steering hub';
  steeringHub.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
  steeringPivot.add(steeringHub);
  for (let spoke = 0; spoke < 3; spoke++) {
    const bar = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.035, 0.22, 0.035), chromeMat);
    bar.userData.studioName = `Steering spoke ${spoke + 1}`;
    bar.rotation.z = spoke * Math.PI * 2 / 3;
    steeringPivot.add(bar);
  }

  const grille = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.42, 0.24, 0.08), carbonMat);
  grille.position.set(0, 0.52, 2.24);
  const splitter = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.08, 0.07, 0.35), carbonMat);
  splitter.position.set(0, 0.32, 2.26);

  const hlMat = makeMat(0xfef9c3, { emissive: 0xfef08a, emissiveIntensity: 0.9 });
  const hlL = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.44, 0.14, 0.08), hlMat);
  hlL.position.set(-0.68, 0.66, 2.24);
  const hlR = hlL.clone();
  hlR.position.x = 0.68;

  const tlMat = makeMat(0xef4444, { emissive: 0xdc2626, emissiveIntensity: 0.65 });
  const tlL = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.56, 0.14, 0.08), tlMat);
  tlL.position.set(-0.64, 0.68, -2.24);
  const tlR = tlL.clone();
  tlR.position.x = 0.64;

  const diffuser = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.88, 0.22, 0.14), carbonMat);
  diffuser.position.set(0, 0.36, -2.23);

  const pipeGeo = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.07, 0.07, 0.22, 10);
  const pipeL = new THREE.Mesh(pipeGeo, chromeMat);
  pipeL.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
  pipeL.position.set(-0.52, 0.34, -2.28);
  const pipeR = pipeL.clone();
  pipeR.position.x = 0.52;

  // Twin Exhaust Flame Cones for Downshift Backfires
  const flameMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xf97316, transparent: true, opacity: 0.92 });
  const flameCoreMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x38bdf8, transparent: true, opacity: 0.95 });
  const flameL = new THREE.Group();
  const fOuterL = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(0.14, 0.95, 8), flameMat);
  fOuterL.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
  fOuterL.position.z = -0.45;
  const fInnerL = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(0.07, 0.52, 8), flameCoreMat);
  fInnerL.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
  fInnerL.position.z = -0.26;
  flameL.add(fOuterL, fInnerL);
  flameL.position.set(-0.52, 0.34, -2.35);
  flameL.visible = false;

  const flameR = flameL.clone();
  flameR.position.x = 0.52;
  flameR.visible = false;

  chassis.add(lower, sideSkirtL, sideSkirtR, stripe, cabin, dashboard, steeringPivot, ...seats, grille, splitter, hlL, hlR, tlL, tlR, diffuser, pipeL, pipeR, flameL, flameR);

  if (isCoupe || isSupercar) {
    const wingY = isSupercar ? 1.02 : 1.04;
    const wing = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(isSupercar ? 2.06 : 1.88, 0.07, 0.34), carbonMat);
    wing.position.set(0, wingY, -2.06);
    const s1 = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.07, 0.24, 0.22), carbonMat);
    s1.position.set(-0.62, wingY - 0.11, -2.06);
    const s2 = s1.clone();
    s2.position.x = 0.62;
    chassis.add(wing, s1, s2);
  }

  const tireMat = makeMat(0x111827, { roughness: 0.85, flatShading: false });
  const rimMat = chromeMat;
  const brakeCaliperMat = makeMat(isSupercar ? 0xef4444 : accentHex, { metalness: 0.7, roughness: 0.25 });
  const wheelPivots = [];
  const wheelSpinners = [];

  [
    [-0.98, 0.38, 1.38],
    [0.98, 0.38, 1.38],
    [-0.98, 0.38, -1.38],
    [0.98, 0.38, -1.38]
  ].forEach(([wx, wy, wz], idx) => {
    const steerPivot = new THREE.Group();
    steerPivot.userData.studioName = `${idx < 2 ? 'Front' : 'Rear'} ${idx % 2 === 0 ? 'left' : 'right'} wheel`;
    steerPivot.position.set(wx, wy, wz);

    const spinGroup = new THREE.Group();
    const tire = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.38, 0.38, 0.28, 18), tireMat);
    tire.rotation.z = Math.PI / 2;
    tire.castShadow = true;

    const rim = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.24, 0.24, 0.295, 14), rimMat);
    rim.rotation.z = Math.PI / 2;

    const caliper = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.16, 0.14, 0.12), brakeCaliperMat);
    caliper.position.set(idx % 2 === 0 ? -0.06 : 0.06, 0.11, -0.09);

    spinGroup.add(tire, rim, caliper);
    steerPivot.add(spinGroup);
    root.add(steerPivot);
    wheelPivots.push(steerPivot);
    wheelSpinners.push(spinGroup);
  });

  return {
    root, chassis, wheelPivots, wheelSpinners, flameL, flameR, windowMaterial: glassMat,
    steeringPivot, name, isSupercar, carScale,
    cameraSeat: new THREE.Vector3(-0.38, isSupercar ? 1.02 : 1.30, 0.12),
    cameraYaw: yaw + Math.PI, cameraPitch: 0.25, cameraLookIdle: 2, lastCameraYaw: yaw
  };
}

export function buildSportsCar(cfg) {
  const { name, x, z, yaw, isSupercar = false } = cfg;
  const {
    root, chassis, wheelPivots, wheelSpinners, flameL, flameR, windowMaterial,
    steeringPivot, carScale, cameraSeat, cameraYaw, cameraPitch, cameraLookIdle, lastCameraYaw
  } = buildSportsCarMesh(cfg);
  worldRoot.add(root);

  const carObj = {
    name,
    isSupercar,
    x,
    y: 0.12,
    z,
    yaw,
    speed: 0,
    velX: 0,
    velZ: 0,
    gearIndex: 2, // 0='R', 1='N', 2='1', ..., 7='6'
    rpm: 900,
    steerAngle: 0,
    pitchAngle: 0,
    rollAngle: 0,
    flameTimer: 0,
    flameL,
    flameR,
    windowMaterial,
    root,
    chassis,
    carScale,
    cameraSeat,
    cameraYaw,
    cameraPitch,
    cameraLookIdle,
    lastCameraYaw,
    steeringPivot,
    wheelPivots,
    wheelSpinners
  };
  vehicles.push(carObj);
  return carObj;
}

export function getNearestVehicle(maxDist = 5.2) {
  let best = null;
  let bestDist = maxDist;
  vehicles.forEach((v, idx) => {
    const d = Math.hypot(player.x - v.x, player.z - v.z);
    if (d < bestDist && Math.abs(player.y - v.y) < 2.2) {
      bestDist = d;
      best = { v, idx };
    }
  });
  return best;
}

export function enterVehicle(idx) {
  const v = vehicles[idx];
  if (!v) return;
  hooks.onVehicleEnter(v);
  gameState.inCar = true;
  gameState.activeCarIndex = idx;
  gameState.isAiming = false;
  inputState.isPrimaryFireHeld = false;
  player.crouching = false;
  player.yaw = v.yaw + Math.PI;
  player.pitch = 0;
  v.lastCameraYaw = v.yaw;
  v.cameraYaw = v.yaw + Math.PI;
  v.cameraPitch = 0.25;
  v.cameraLookIdle = 2;
  v.gearIndex = 2; // Start in 1st Gear
  v.rpm = 1100;
  sfx.shift();
  pushFeed(`ENTERED ${v.name} â€” [I] UPSHIFT â€¢ [K] DOWNSHIFT`, 'border-l-amber-400');
  hooks.syncMobileControls(true);
  hooks.updateHUD();
}

export function exitCurrentCar(silent = false) {
  if (!gameState.inCar) return;
  const v = vehicles[gameState.activeCarIndex];
  gameState.inCar = false;
  gameState.activeCarIndex = -1;
  if (v) {
    v.flameL.visible = false;
    v.flameR.visible = false;
    const exitDistance = 2.1 * (v.carScale || 1.35);
    const forwardX = Math.sin(v.yaw);
    const forwardZ = Math.cos(v.yaw);
    const candidates = [
      { x: v.x - Math.cos(v.yaw) * exitDistance, z: v.z + Math.sin(v.yaw) * exitDistance },
      { x: v.x + Math.cos(v.yaw) * exitDistance, z: v.z - Math.sin(v.yaw) * exitDistance },
      { x: v.x - forwardX * exitDistance, z: v.z - forwardZ * exitDistance },
      { x: v.x, y: v.y + 3, z: v.z }
    ];
    let exitSpot = null;
    for (const candidate of candidates) {
      const candidateY = candidate.y ?? Math.max(0.18, v.y + 0.06);
      const floorY = computeSupportFloor(candidate.x, candidate.z, candidateY + 0.4, player.radius);
      if (!collidesHorizontally(candidate.x, candidate.z, floorY, player.height, player.radius)) {
        exitSpot = { x: candidate.x, y: candidate.y ?? Math.max(floorY, v.y + 0.06), z: candidate.z };
        break;
      }
    }
    exitSpot ||= { x: v.x, y: Math.max(0.18, v.y + 0.06), z: v.z };
    player.x = exitSpot.x;
    player.y = exitSpot.y;
    player.z = exitSpot.z;
    player.vy = 0;
    player.grounded = true;
    player.peakAirY = player.y;
    player.yaw = v.yaw + Math.PI;
    player.pitch = 0;
  }
  sfx.updateEngine(false, 0, false);
  if (!silent) pushFeed('EXITED VEHICLE', 'border-l-slate-400');
  hooks.syncMobileControls(true);
  hooks.updateHUD();
}

export function shiftVehicleGear(delta) {
  if (!gameState.inCar) return;
  const v = vehicles[gameState.activeCarIndex];
  if (!v) return;
  const next = Math.max(0, Math.min(GEAR_LABELS.length - 1, v.gearIndex + delta));
  if (next !== v.gearIndex) {
    const prevGear = v.gearIndex;
    v.gearIndex = next;
    sfx.shift();
    if (delta > 0 && next >= 2) {
      v.rpm = Math.max(1400, v.rpm * 0.68);
    } else if (delta < 0 && next >= 2) {
      v.rpm = Math.min(7950, v.rpm * 1.38 + 900);
      // Supercar & high-RPM downshift exhaust backfire flames!
      if (v.isSupercar || prevGear >= 4) {
        v.flameTimer = v.isSupercar ? 0.52 : 0.28;
        sfx.backfire();
        spawnParticles(
          v.x - Math.sin(v.yaw) * 2.4,
          v.y + 0.36,
          v.z - Math.cos(v.yaw) * 2.4,
          0xf97316,
          v.isSupercar ? 10 : 5,
          4.5
        );
      }
    }
    pushFeed(`SHIFTED TO GEAR ${GEAR_LABELS[v.gearIndex]}`, 'border-l-amber-400');
  }
}

// Per-frame driving model (6-speed gearbox, grip, collisions, camera). Extracted from the original stepSimulation().
export function stepVehicleDriving(dt, elapsed) {
    const v = vehicles[gameState.activeCarIndex];
    const throttleInput = keys['KeyW'] || keys['ArrowUp'] ? 1 : 0;
    const brakeInput = keys['KeyS'] || keys['ArrowDown'] ? 1 : 0;
    const steerInput = (keys['KeyA'] || keys['ArrowLeft'] ? 1 : 0) - (keys['KeyD'] || keys['ArrowRight'] ? 1 : 0);
    const handbrake = keys['Space'] ? 1 : 0;

    const gIdx = v.gearIndex;
    const gearCapMps = GEAR_CAPS_MPH[gIdx] / 2.237 * (v.isTank ? 0.48 : 1);
    const gearRatio = GEAR_RATIO_MULT[gIdx];
    const prevSpeed = v.speed;

    // Update Supercar / Backfire Exhaust Flame Effect
    if (v.flameTimer > 0) {
      v.flameTimer -= dt;
      const flOn = v.flameTimer > 0 && Math.sin(elapsed * 65) > -0.35;
      v.flameL.visible = flOn;
      v.flameR.visible = flOn;
      if (flOn) {
        const s = 0.8 + Math.random() * 0.65;
        v.flameL.scale.set(1, 1, s);
        v.flameR.scale.set(1, 1, s);
      }
    } else {
      v.flameL.visible = false;
      v.flameR.visible = false;
    }

    let onLimiter = false;
    if (gIdx === 1) {
      if (throttleInput) v.rpm = Math.min(7900, v.rpm + 6400 * dt);
      else v.rpm = Math.max(880, v.rpm - 4400 * dt);
      v.speed *= Math.max(0, 1 - 0.45 * dt);
    } else if (gIdx === 0) {
      const targetRpm = 900 + (Math.abs(v.speed) / Math.max(1, gearCapMps)) * 6600;
      v.rpm += (targetRpm - v.rpm) * Math.min(1, dt * 10);
      if (throttleInput) {
        v.speed = Math.max(-gearCapMps, v.speed - 14.5 * dt);
      } else {
        v.speed *= Math.max(0, 1 - 1.5 * dt);
      }
    } else {
      const speedRatio = Math.max(0, v.speed / Math.max(1, gearCapMps));
      const rawRpm = 900 + speedRatio * 6950;
      v.rpm += (Math.min(8000, rawRpm) - v.rpm) * Math.min(1, dt * 11);

      const normRpm = v.rpm / 8000;
      const torqueCurve = Math.max(0.32, 1 - Math.pow((normRpm - 0.62) / 0.65, 2));
      const superMult = v.isTank ? 0.62 : v.isSupercar ? 1.32 : 1.0;
      const driveAccel = 23.0 * superMult * gearRatio * torqueCurve * levelData.params.vehicles.accelMultiplier;

      if (throttleInput) {
        if (v.speed < gearCapMps) {
          v.speed = Math.min(gearCapMps, v.speed + driveAccel * dt);
        } else {
          onLimiter = true;
          v.rpm = 7750 + Math.sin(elapsed * 55) * 240;
          v.speed -= 4.0 * dt;
        }
      } else {
        const engineBrake = 2.4 + (1.45 / Math.max(0.2, gearRatio)) * 0.55;
        v.speed = Math.max(0, v.speed - engineBrake * dt);
      }
    }

    if (brakeInput) {
      if (v.speed > 0.2) v.speed = Math.max(0, v.speed - 36.0 * levelData.params.vehicles.brakeMultiplier * dt);
      else if (v.speed < -0.2) v.speed = Math.min(0, v.speed + 36.0 * levelData.params.vehicles.brakeMultiplier * dt);
      else v.speed = 0;
    }
    if (handbrake) {
      v.speed *= Math.max(0, 1 - (v.isSupercar ? 2.1 : 3.2) * dt);
    }

    const targetSteer = steerInput * (v.isSupercar ? 0.54 : 0.46) * levelData.params.vehicles.steerMultiplier;
    v.steerAngle += (targetSteer - v.steerAngle) * Math.min(1, dt * 11);

    const speedFactor = Math.min(1, Math.abs(v.speed) / 4.2);
    const dirSign = v.speed >= 0 ? 1 : -1;
    const driftYawMult = handbrake ? (v.isSupercar ? 1.55 : 1.32) : 1.0;
    v.yaw += v.steerAngle * 2.45 * speedFactor * dirSign * driftYawMult * dt;

    const headX = Math.sin(v.yaw);
    const headZ = Math.cos(v.yaw);
    const targetVx = headX * v.speed;
    const targetVz = headZ * v.speed;
    const lateralGrip = handbrake ? (v.isSupercar ? 1.35 : 1.8) : (v.isSupercar ? 8.2 : 6.8);
    const gripMul = Math.min(1, surfaceGrip(v.x, v.z) / 14 + 0.12) * levelData.params.vehicles.gripMultiplier;
    if (isInWater(v.x, v.z)) v.speed *= Math.max(0, 1 - 1.4 * dt);
    v.velX += (targetVx - v.velX) * Math.min(1, lateralGrip * gripMul * dt);
    v.velZ += (targetVz - v.velZ) * Math.min(1, lateralGrip * gripMul * dt);

    const slipSpeed = Math.hypot(v.velX - targetVx, v.velZ - targetVz);
    const isSkidding = (handbrake && Math.abs(v.speed) > 4.5) || (slipSpeed > 3.6 && Math.abs(v.speed) > 6.5) || (brakeInput && Math.abs(v.speed) > 12);
    if (isSkidding) {
      sfx.skid(dt);
      const rWheelX = v.x - headX * 1.4;
      const rWheelZ = v.z - headZ * 1.4;
      spawnParticles(rWheelX, v.y + 0.14, rWheelZ, handbrake && v.isSupercar ? 0xf8fafc : 0xcbd5e1, 1, 1.8);
    }

    const nextX = v.x + v.velX * dt;
    const nextZ = v.z + v.velZ * dt;

    const cfx = Math.sin(v.yaw), cfz = Math.cos(v.yaw);
    const crx = Math.cos(v.yaw), crz = -Math.sin(v.yaw);
    const carFloor = computeSupportFloor(nextX, nextZ, v.y + 0.65, 0.8);
    const wheelSupport = (forward, side) => computeSupportFloor(
      nextX + cfx * forward * v.carScale + crx * side * v.carScale,
      nextZ + cfz * forward * v.carScale + crz * side * v.carScale,
      v.y + 0.65,
      0.42
    );
    const frontFloor = (wheelSupport(1.38, -0.98) + wheelSupport(1.38, 0.98)) * 0.5;
    const rearFloor = (wheelSupport(-1.38, -0.98) + wheelSupport(-1.38, 0.98)) * 0.5;
    const gy = Math.max(carFloor, frontFloor, rearFloor);
    const groundY = gy + 0.02;
    v.vy = v.vy || 0;
    if (groundY > v.y + 0.08) {
      v.vy = Math.max(0, v.vy);
      v.y = groundY;
    } else if (groundY < v.y) {
      v.vy -= 19.5 * dt;
      v.y += v.vy * dt;
      if (v.y <= groundY) {
        v.y = groundY;
        v.vy = 0;
      }
    } else {
      v.vy = 0;
      v.y = groundY;
    }
    const tpT = Math.max(-0.5, Math.min(0.5, Math.atan2(frontFloor - rearFloor, 3.72 * v.carScale)));
    v.terrPitch = (v.terrPitch || 0) + (tpT - (v.terrPitch || 0)) * Math.min(1, dt * 10);

    for (const lp of lampPosts) {
      if (lp.state === 'standing' && Math.hypot(nextX - lp.x, nextZ - lp.z) < 1.85 && Math.abs(v.y - 0.18) < 2.0) {
        hooks.knockOverLamppost(lp, v.yaw, Math.abs(v.speed));
        v.speed *= 0.88;
      }
    }

    for (const npc of npcs) {
if (npc.spawned && !npc.asleep && Math.hypot(nextX - npc.x, nextZ - npc.z) < 1.85 && Math.abs(v.speed) > 2.2 && Math.abs(v.y - 0.18) < 2.0) {
        hooks.damageNpc(npc, Math.abs(v.speed) * 4.5, v.yaw);
        sfx.hit();
        spawnParticles(npc.x, 1.1, npc.z, 0xfacc15, 7, 5.0);
        v.speed *= 0.85;
      }
    }

    const carHit = (px, pz) => {
      for (let q = 0; q < CAR_PTS.length; q++) {
        if (collidesHorizontally(px + cfx * CAR_PTS[q][0] + crx * CAR_PTS[q][1], pz + cfz * CAR_PTS[q][0] + crz * CAR_PTS[q][1], v.y + 0.25, 1.3, 0.28)) return true;
      }
      return false;
    };
    let okX = true, okZ = true;
    const nSub = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(Math.hypot(nextX - v.x, nextZ - v.z) / 0.45));
    const stX = (nextX - v.x) / nSub, stZ = (nextZ - v.z) / nSub;
    for (let si = 0; si < nSub && (okX || okZ); si++) {
      if (okX) { if (carHit(v.x + stX, v.z)) okX = false; else v.x += stX; }
      if (okZ) { if (carHit(v.x, v.z + stZ)) okZ = false; else v.z += stZ; }
    }
    if (!okX) v.velX *= -0.28;
    if (!okZ) v.velZ *= -0.28;
    if (!okX || !okZ) {
      const impact = Math.abs(v.speed);
      v.speed *= (okX || okZ) ? 0.82 : -0.3;
      if (impact > 5) {
        sfx.hit();
        spawnParticles(v.x, v.y + 0.7, v.z, 0xfbbf24, 6, 4);
        if (impact > 14) hooks.applyPlayerDamage(Math.min(30, (impact - 14) * 1.6), 'CRASH IMPACT');
      }
    }

    const accelDelta = (v.speed - prevSpeed) / Math.max(0.008, dt);
    const targetPitch = Math.max(-0.06, Math.min(0.06, -accelDelta * 0.0022));
    const targetRoll = Math.max(-0.08, Math.min(0.08, v.steerAngle * (v.speed / 28) * 0.14));
    v.pitchAngle += (targetPitch - v.pitchAngle) * Math.min(1, dt * 10);
    v.rollAngle += (targetRoll - v.rollAngle) * Math.min(1, dt * 10);

    v.root.position.set(v.x, v.y, v.z);
    v.root.rotation.y = v.yaw;
    v.chassis.rotation.x = v.pitchAngle - (v.terrPitch || 0);
    v.chassis.rotation.z = v.rollAngle;

    v.wheelPivots[0].rotation.y = v.steerAngle;
    v.wheelPivots[1].rotation.y = v.steerAngle;
    const wheelRot = (v.speed * dt) / 0.38;
    v.wheelSpinners.forEach((ws) => { ws.rotation.x += wheelRot; });

    sfx.updateEngine(true, v.rpm, Boolean(throttleInput), onLimiter);

    player.x = v.x;
    player.y = v.y + 0.06;
    player.z = v.z;

    if (!gameState.thirdPerson) {
      let yawStep = v.yaw - (v.lastCameraYaw === undefined ? v.yaw : v.lastCameraYaw);
      while (yawStep > Math.PI) yawStep -= Math.PI * 2;
      while (yawStep < -Math.PI) yawStep += Math.PI * 2;
      player.yaw += yawStep;
      v.lastCameraYaw = v.yaw;
      camera.position.copy(v.root.localToWorld(v.cameraSeat.clone()));
      camera.rotation.y = player.yaw;
      camera.rotation.x = player.pitch;
      v.steeringPivot.rotation.z = -v.steerAngle * 1.7;
    } else {
      v.cameraLookIdle = (v.cameraLookIdle || 0) + dt;
      if (v.cameraLookIdle > 2) {
        const followYaw = v.yaw + Math.PI;
        const yawDelta = Math.atan2(Math.sin(followYaw - v.cameraYaw), Math.cos(followYaw - v.cameraYaw));
        const followBlend = 1 - Math.exp(-dt * 1.8);
        v.cameraYaw += yawDelta * followBlend;
        v.cameraPitch += (0.25 - v.cameraPitch) * followBlend;
      }
      const aerialDist = (11.4 * (v.carScale || 1.35)) + Math.min(5.0, Math.abs(v.speed) * 0.085);
      const horizontalDist = aerialDist * Math.cos(v.cameraPitch);
      const camX = v.x + Math.sin(v.cameraYaw) * horizontalDist;
      const camZ = v.z + Math.cos(v.cameraYaw) * horizontalDist;
      const camY = Math.max(
        v.y + 1.15 + Math.sin(v.cameraPitch) * aerialDist,
        terrainH(camX, camZ) + 3.0,
        2.4
      );
      camera.position.set(camX, camY, camZ);
      camera.lookAt(v.x, v.y + 0.95, v.z);
    }
}

// ---- Car suspension & terrain physics ----
export function suspFrame(dt, t) {
for (const v of vehicles) {
const ch = v.chassis; if (!ch) continue;
const S = v.sus || (v.sus = { hy: 0, hv: 0, p: 0, pv: 0, r: 0, rv: 0, pa: 0, ps: v.speed || 0, lat: 0 });
const driven = gameState.inCar && vehicles[gameState.activeCarIndex] === v;
const acc = (v.speed - S.ps) / Math.max(dt, .001); S.ps = v.speed;
const lat = -(v.steerAngle || 0) * v.speed * Math.abs(v.speed) * .03;
const bump = (Math.sin(v.x * 1.7 + v.z * 1.3) * .5 + Math.sin(v.x * .37) * .5) * Math.min(1, Math.abs(v.speed) / 12) * (v.z < -170 ? .9 : .35);
const k = 70, c = 9;
S.hv += (-k * S.hy - c * S.hv + bump * 30) * dt; S.hy += S.hv * dt;
S.pv += (-k * .9 * S.p - c * .8 * S.pv - Math.max(-14, Math.min(14, acc)) * .07 * k * .25) * dt; S.p += S.pv * dt;
S.rv += (-k * .9 * S.r - c * .8 * S.rv + Math.max(-14, Math.min(14, lat)) * .02 * k) * dt; S.r += S.rv * dt;
const sx = Math.sin(v.yaw), cz = Math.cos(v.yaw);
const slope = Math.atan2(terrainH(v.x + sx * 2, v.z + cz * 2) - terrainH(v.x - sx * 2, v.z - cz * 2), 4);
S.pa += ((v.z < -170 ? slope : 0) - S.pa) * Math.min(1, dt * 8);
ch.position.y = S.hy * .35;
if (driven) {
ch.rotation.x = (v.pitchAngle || 0) - (v.terrPitch || 0) + S.p * .5 - S.pa;
ch.rotation.z = (v.rollAngle || 0) + S.r * .5;
} else {
ch.rotation.x = S.p * .5 - S.pa;
ch.rotation.z = S.r * .5;
}
v.root.rotation.order = 'YXZ';
v.root.rotation.x = -S.pa;
if (v.z < -170 && v.speed > 0) v.speed = Math.max(0, v.speed - 9.8 * Math.sin(Math.max(0, S.pa)) * dt * .9);
}
}

// Helper: Builds a wall segment with a real rectangular window cutout & real glass pane!
export function buildWallWithRealWindow(parent, width, height, thick, winW, winH, winCenterY, wallMat, addMullions = true) {
  const group = new THREE.Group();
  const sideW = Math.max(0.1, (width - winW) / 2);
  const botH = Math.max(0.1, winCenterY - winH / 2);
  const topH = Math.max(0.1, height - (winCenterY + winH / 2));

  const leftWall = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(sideW, height, thick), wallMat);
  leftWall.position.set(-(winW / 2 + sideW / 2), height / 2, 0);
  leftWall.castShadow = true;
  leftWall.receiveShadow = true;

  const rightWall = leftWall.clone();
  rightWall.position.x = winW / 2 + sideW / 2;

  const botWall = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(winW, botH, thick), wallMat);
  botWall.position.set(0, botH / 2, 0);
  botWall.castShadow = true;
  botWall.receiveShadow = true;

  const topWall = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(winW, topH, thick), wallMat);
  topWall.position.set(0, winCenterY + winH / 2 + topH / 2, 0);
  topWall.castShadow = true;

  // Real Transparent Glass Pane
  const glassPane = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(winW, winH), realWindowGlassMat);
  glassPane.position.set(0, winCenterY, 0);

  // Window Sill & Frame Trim
  const sill = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(winW + 0.16, 0.08, thick + 0.12), makeMat(0xf8fafc));
  sill.position.set(0, winCenterY - winH / 2, 0);

  group.add(leftWall, rightWall, botWall, topWall, glassPane, sill);

  if (addMullions) {
    const mH = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(winW, 0.05, 0.06), makeMat(0xf8fafc));
    mH.position.set(0, winCenterY, 0);
    const mV = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.05, winH, 0.06), makeMat(0xf8fafc));
    mV.position.set(0, winCenterY, 0);
    group.add(mH, mV);
  }

  parent.add(group);
  return group;
}

export const sculptableTerrainMeshes = [];

function registerSculptableTerrain(mesh) {
  const positions = mesh.geometry.attributes.position;
  mesh.userData.terrainBaseY = Float32Array.from(positions.array.filter((_, index) => index % 3 === 1));
  const bounds = new THREE.Box3().setFromBufferAttribute(positions);
  mesh.userData.terrainBounds = {
    minX: bounds.min.x + mesh.position.x, maxX: bounds.max.x + mesh.position.x,
    minZ: bounds.min.z + mesh.position.z, maxZ: bounds.max.z + mesh.position.z
  };
  mesh.userData.sculptableTerrain = true;
  sculptableTerrainMeshes.push(mesh);
  refreshTerrainSculpt(mesh.position.x, mesh.position.z, Infinity, mesh);
  return mesh;
}

/** Update visible terrain meshes after a sculpt stroke or undo/redo. */
export function refreshTerrainSculpt(centerX = null, centerZ = null, radius = Infinity, onlyMesh = null) {
  const meshes = onlyMesh ? [onlyMesh] : sculptableTerrainMeshes;
  for (const mesh of meshes) {
    const positions = mesh.geometry.attributes.position;
    const baseY = mesh.userData.terrainBaseY;
    const partial = Number.isFinite(radius);
    const bounds = mesh.userData.terrainBounds;
    if (partial && (centerX + radius < bounds.minX - 3 || centerX - radius > bounds.maxX + 3
      || centerZ + radius < bounds.minZ - 3 || centerZ - radius > bounds.maxZ + 3)) continue;
    let changed = false;
    for (let i = 0; i < positions.count; i++) {
      const x = mesh.position.x + positions.getX(i), z = mesh.position.z + positions.getZ(i);
      if (partial && Math.hypot(x - centerX, z - centerZ) > radius + 3) continue;
      positions.setY(i, baseY[i] + terrainHeightOffset(x, z));
      changed = true;
    }
    if (changed) {
      positions.needsUpdate = true;
      mesh.geometry.computeVertexNormals();
      mesh.geometry.computeBoundingSphere();
    }
  }
}

function terrainPlane(width, depth, x, y, z, material) {
  const geometry = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(width, depth, Math.ceil(width / 2), Math.ceil(depth / 2));
  geometry.rotateX(-Math.PI / 2);
  const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(geometry, material);
  mesh.position.set(x, y, z);
  mesh.receiveShadow = true;
  registerSculptableTerrain(mesh);
  return mesh;
}
